            drivingControlRegion.Out_CcState,
            sys_brakeInput.pressure,
            safetyMonitoringRegion.Out_ConfirmedFaultCause,
            sys_torqueReq.requestedTorqueDelta
        );
        // 모드 채널 되먹임: 첫 주기 초기값. CF1은 직전 주기에 방송된 모드를 본다 (1주기 지연).
        // 모드는 플래그(cf2_active, cf3_faultActive)로만 정해져 CF1 출력에 의존하지 않으므로 대수 루프는 없다 (S2).
        entry action {
            assign modeChannelBroadcast.currentMode := SystemOperatingMode::Normal_Mode;
            assign modeChannelBroadcast.inhibitDrivingControl := false;
        }

        bind egas.ecu.activeInterventionSource = emergencyInterventionRegion.Out_ActiveSource; // M5 관측 속성

        // 상태 전이 시 충돌 방지를 위한 이벤트 우선순위 중재 제약
        constraint RaceConditionResolutionAndPriority {
            doc
            /* [중재 논리]
             * 동일한 제어 주기(systemClockCycle) 내에 외부 액터의 제동 입력(sys_brakeInput)과 
             * 내부 Arbiter의 안전(FailSafe) 차단 브로드캐스트(inhibitDrivingControl == true)가 
             * 동시에 발생(Simultaneous Events)할 수 있습니다.
             * 시스템의 물리적 안전 보장이 최우선이므로, 상태 머신은 브레이크 오버라이드(Brake Override)로 전이하는 것보다 
             * Mode Channel의 강제 대기(Forced Inhibited Standby) 차단 신호를 절대적으로 우선(Strictly Higher Priority)하여 평가하고 처리해야 합니다.
             * 제어 경로(페달, 크루즈)는 systemClockCycle 주기로 갱신되고 반응 경로(오버라이드, 개입, 고착 차단, 페일세이프, 보정)는 입력 이벤트로 즉시 처리된다(RV_InterfaceModel (C) 샘플링 지연 상수 참조).
             */
            modeChannelBroadcast.inhibitDrivingControl == true implies
            SafeStateLogic::IsFullCutOff(sys_airCmd.massFlow, sys_fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth)
        }

        constraint BrakeOverrideSystemLevel {
            // SHR-01의 시스템 통합 수준 보장 (모드와 무관)
            doc
            /* 구동 요구 중 제동 입력이 있으면 Normal/Emergency/FailSafe 어느 모드에서도 최종 출력은 L1 이내여야 한다. */
            SafeStateLogic::IsBrakeOverrideTriggered(
                sys_accelInput.pedalDisplacement,
                drivingControlRegion.Out_CcState,
                sys_brakeInput.pressure
            ) implies
            SafeStateLogic::IsTorqueLimited(sys_airCmd.massFlow, sys_fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth)
        }

        // 시스템 기동 시 모든 영역이 병렬로 실행됨 (Parallel Regions)
        // 1. 주행 토크 제어 상태 머신 (Extended 버전으로 교체)
        state redefines drivingControlRegion : ExtendedDrivingTorqueBehavior {
            bind In_Ignition = sys_ignition;
            bind In_CombustionSystemStatus = sys_combustionStatus;
            bind In_Accel = sys_accelInput;
            bind In_Brake = sys_brakeInput;
            bind In_CcCmd = sys_ccCmd;
            bind In_VehicleDynamics = sys_dynamicsStatus;
            bind In_NormalDriving = normalDriving;

            // Arbiter가 출력한 modeChannel 신호를 입력으로 받음
            bind In_ModeCtrl = modeChannelBroadcast;
            bind In_Sensor = sys_sensorData;
        }

        // 2. 긴급 토크 개입 상태 머신
        state
            redefines emergencyInterventionRegion
            : FE_BehaviorModel::EmergencyInterventionBehavior {
            // 시스템 경계의 피드백 데이터를 내부로 바인딩
            bind In_ThrottleCmd = sys_throttleCmd;
            bind In_TorqueReq = sys_torqueReq;
        }

        // 3. 안전 감시 상태 머신
        state redefines safetyMonitoringRegion : FE_BehaviorModel::SafetyMonitoringBehavior {
            // 이중화 센서 데이터 및 연소 상태 피드백 바인딩
            bind In_Sensor = sys_sensorData;
            bind In_PedalPosition = sys_pedalPosition;
            bind In_ThrottleFeedback = sys_throttleFeedback;
            bind In_Ignition = sys_ignition; // 래치 해제에 필요
            bind Out_Dashboard = sys_dashboard;
        }
        state
            redefines
                monitoringAndServiceRegion.monitoringActive.calibrationHandling
            : FE_BehaviorModel::CalibrationSupportBehavior {
            bind In_Ignition = sys_ignition;
            bind In_VehicleSpeed = sys_dynamicsStatus.vehicleSpeed; // S9
            bind In_ExpectedCredential = egas.ecu.expectedAccessCredential;
            bind In_CalReq = sys_calReq;

            bind Out_CalRes = sys_calStatus;
        }
        state
            redefines
                monitoringAndServiceRegion.monitoringActive.diagnosticsHandling
            : FE_BehaviorModel::DiagnosticsAndMaintenanceSupportBehavior {
            // 진단/유지보수 영역에도 센서 데이터 바인딩 적용
            bind In_FaultMemory = safetyMonitoringRegion.Out_FaultMemory; // Before: In_FaultCause = ...Out_ConfirmedFaultCause
            bind In_Ignition = sys_ignition;
            bind In_VehicleSpeed = sys_dynamicsStatus.vehicleSpeed;
            bind In_DiagReq = sys_diagReq;
            bind Out_DiagRes = sys_diagStatus;
        }

        // 다른 상태 영역들(drivingControlRegion 등)과 병렬로 상시 동작하며
        // 하위 시스템들의 출력을 모아 우선순위를 평가하고 최종 출력을 내보냅니다.
        state ModeArbitrationRegion {
            // 이 영역 내에서 Arbiter action을 상시 실행 (Do-behavior)
            do action systemArbiter : Arbiter {
                // 각 상태 영역에서 산출된 값을 Arbiter의 입력으로 바인딩 (기존과 동일)
                bind cf1_airCmd = drivingControlRegion.Out_AirCmd;
                bind cf1_fuelCmd = drivingControlRegion.Out_FuelCmd;

                bind cf2_airCmd = emergencyInterventionRegion.Out_AirCmd;
                bind cf2_fuelCmd = emergencyInterventionRegion.Out_FuelCmd;
                bind cf2_active = emergencyInterventionRegion.Out_Active;

                bind cf3_airCmd = safetyMonitoringRegion.Out_AirCmd;
                bind cf3_fuelCmd = safetyMonitoringRegion.Out_FuelCmd;
                bind cf3_faultActive = safetyMonitoringRegion.Out_FaultActive;

                // Arbiter의 최종 결정값을 기존의 내부 변수(finalAirCmd)가 아닌 외부 시스템 경계 출력(sys_airCmd)으로 다이렉트 바인딩
                bind final_airCmd = sys_airCmd;
                bind final_fuelCmd = sys_fuelCmd;
                bind modeChannelBroadcaster = modeChannelBroadcast;
            }
        }

        state redefines historyLatchRegion {
            transition historyTick
                first latching
                accept after TimingConceptsAndLimits::systemClockCycle
                do action {
                    assign egas.ecu.lastAirMassFlow := sys_airCmd.massFlow; // 직전 최종 출력 기록
                    assign egas.ecu.lastFuelPulseWidth := sys_fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth;
                }
                then latching {
                dependency self to t_historyTick;
            }
        }
    }
} // package FE_SystemBehaviorModel

##02. Functional Viewpoint/3-1. FW UF1 WhiteBox Model.sysml##
package FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel {
    doc
    /* [CF1 주행 토크 제어 - 화이트박스 모델 파트 1]
     * 본 패키지는 UF1 기능 블랙박스(FB)의 각 원자적 유저 기능 내부를
     * Sensing -> Core Logic -> Actuation의 3단계 파이프라인으로 분해합니다.
     * 상위 제약조건의 중복 선언을 배제하고, 하위 기능으로의 제약 할당(Budgeting) 및
     * 내부 데이터 흐름(Internal Flow) 로직만을 명세하여 결합도를 낮추고 응집도를 높였습니다.
     */

    private import FB_FunctionalBlackBoxModel::*;
    private import FB_ModeArbitrationModel::*;
    private import RV_InterfaceModel::*;
    private import ReqCommon::*;
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import TimingConceptsAndLimits::*;

    @MetaInformation {
        version = "v0.3";
    }

    // ==========================================
    //  테스트 오라클 검증을 위한 메타데이터 정의
    // ==========================================
    metadata def TestOracle {
        doc
        /* 테스트 오라클 속성 정의: 
         * 해당 제약조건이 단독 소프트웨어 시뮬레이션(MiL/SiL)으로 검증 가능한지, 
         * 아니면 물리 플랜트(FMU 등)와의 연동(Co-Simulation)이 필수적인지 명시합니다.
         */
        attribute requiresCoSimulation : Boolean;
        attribute plantModelDependency : String;
        attribute description : String;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 0. 화이트박스 내부 공용 데이터 타입 (Internal Items)
    // ==========================================
    item def InternalTorqueDemand {
        // 내부 논리 연산을 위해 산출된 타겟 물리 토크량
        attribute targetTorque : TorqueValue;
        attribute calculationTimestamp : TimeValue;
    }

    item def InternalErrorData {
        attribute frictionError : Real;
        attribute latencyError : TimeValue;
    }
    item def InternalCorrectionCmd {
        attribute targetAirAdjust : ISQ::MassFlowRateValue;
        attribute targetFuelAdjust : ISQ::DurationValue;
    }

    item def InternalCutoffSignal {
        attribute isCutoffActive : Boolean; // 제동 우선순위에 따른 강제 차단 활성화 플래그
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 1. UF1_1 수동 가속 제어 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF1_1_WB> ManualAccelerationControl_WhiteBox :> CF1::CF1_1 {
        doc
        /* 상위 블랙박스(CF1::UF1_1)의 포트 및 제약조건을 상속받아 내부 흐름을 구현합니다. */

        // 명시적 가시성을 위해 상속받은 in/out 파라미터를 다시 선언 (bind 및 flow 연결용)
        in ignition : IgnitionState :>> CF1::CF1_1::ignition;
        in accel : AccelerationIntent :>> CF1::CF1_1::accel;
        in combustionSystemStatus : CombustionSystemStatus :>> CF1::CF1_1::combustionSystemStatus; // [M3]
        in dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback :>> CF1::CF1_1::dynamics;
        in normalDriving : Boolean :>> CF1::CF1_1::normalDriving;
        out airCmd : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF1::CF1_1::airCmd;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF1::CF1_1::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 페달 입력 필터링 소기능
        action filterPedalInput {
            in rawAccel : AccelerationIntent;
            out validAccel : AccelerationIntent;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // [로컬 제약] 입력 센싱 단계의 물리적 노이즈 제거 필터링에 허용된 최대 지연시간 (예산 10ms)
            constraint Local_FilterLatency { executionTime <= 5.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        // [Step 2: Core Logic] 목표 토크 산출 소기능
        action calcManualTorque {
            in ignStatus : IgnitionState;
            in filteredAccel : AccelerationIntent;
            out torqueDemand : InternalTorqueDemand;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // [로컬 제약] 페달 값을 물리적 목표 토크로 변환하는 코어 알고리즘 연산 지연시간 (예산 20ms)
            constraint Local_CalcLatency { executionTime <= 10.0 [milli * s] }
            // [로컬 제약] 상위 블랙박스의 Precondition을 연산 단계의 필수 선행 조건으로 할당 (시동 On & 페달 변위 > 0)
            constraint Local_Precondition {
                ignStatus.status == IgnitionStatusEnum::On and
                filteredAccel.pedalDisplacement > 0.0 [mm]
            }
        }

        // [Step 3: Actuation] 엔진 제어 명령 변환 소기능
        action genEngineCommand {
            in torqueReq : InternalTorqueDemand;
            out outAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out outFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // [로컬 제약] 산출된 토크를 스로틀/인젝터 제어 펄스로 변환하는 액추에이션 지연시간 (예산 20ms)
            constraint Local_CmdGenLatency { executionTime <= combustionComputeBudget }
        }

        attribute pipelineLatency : TimeValue =
            filterPedalInput.executionTime +
            calcManualTorque.executionTime +
            genEngineCommand.executionTime;

        assert constraint redefines PerformanceConstraints {
            out redefines result =
                airCmd.responseTime == pipelineLatency and
                fuelCmd.responseTime == pipelineLatency and
                pipelineLatency <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::cf1PathBudget;
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 (Internal Flows & Bindings) ---

        // 1. 외부 입력을 필터링 단계와 연산 단계로 전달
        bind accel = filterPedalInput.rawAccel;
        bind ignition = calcManualTorque.ignStatus;

        // 2. 소기능 간의 직렬 파이프라인 연결
        flow filterPedalInput.validAccel to calcManualTorque.filteredAccel;
        flow calcManualTorque.torqueDemand to genEngineCommand.torqueReq;

        // 3. 최종 산출물을 외부 출력으로 바인딩
        bind genEngineCommand.outAir = airCmd;
        bind genEngineCommand.outFuel = fuelCmd;

        @TestOracle {
            requiresCoSimulation = true;
            plantModelDependency = "Engine_Dynamics_Plant_FMU_v2.0";
            description =
                "토크 정상 상태 오차/오버슛과 종방향 Jerk는 소프트웨어 로직만으로 판별 불가. 차량/엔진 플랜트 연동(Co-Simulation) 환경에서 측정되는 오라클입니다.";
        }

        assert constraint redefines TorqueTrackingObservation {
            out redefines result =
                combustionSystemStatus.steadyStateError <= torqueSteadyStateErrorLimitPct and
                combustionSystemStatus.torqueOvershoot <= torqueOvershootLimitPct;
        }
        assert constraint redefines RideComfortObservation {
            out redefines result =
                normalDriving implies dynamics.longitudinalJerk <= longitudinalJerkLimit;
        }
    }

    action def <UF1_1_Ext_WB> ManualAccelerationDriving_ModeAware_WhiteBox :> UF1_1_Ext, UF1_1_WB {
        in modeCtrl : ModeChannelData :>> UF1_1_Ext::modeCtrl;

        action redefines genEngineCommand {
            in inhibit : Boolean;
            constraint Local_InhibitCut {
                inhibit implies
                SafeStateLogic::IsFullCutOff(outAir.massFlow, outFuel.injectionPulseWidth)
            }
        }
        assert constraint redefines ModeInhibitConstraint {
            out redefines result =
                modeCtrl.inhibitDrivingControl == true implies
                SafeStateLogic::IsFullCutOff(airCmd.massFlow, fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth);
        }
        bind modeCtrl.inhibitDrivingControl = genEngineCommand.inhibit;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 2. UF1_2 정속 주행 제어(크루즈) 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF1_2_WB> CruiseControlDriving_WhiteBox :> CF1::CF1_2 {
        in targetSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue :>> CF1::CF1_2::targetSpeed;
        in dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback :>> CF1::CF1_2::dynamics;
        in combustionSystemStatus : CombustionSystemStatus :>> CF1::CF1_2::combustionSystemStatus;
        out airCmd : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF1::CF1_2::airCmd;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF1::CF1_2::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 설정 속도 검증
        action validateTarget {
            in rawTarget : ISQ::SpeedValue;
            out validTarget : ISQ::SpeedValue;
            constraint Local_Precondition { rawTarget > 0.0 [km / h] }
        }

        // [Step 2: Core Logic] 속도 오차 기반 PID 목표 토크 연산
        action calcCruiseTorque {
            in validTarget : ISQ::SpeedValue;
            in vehicleSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue; // 속도 오차 계산의 피드백
            in engineStatus : CombustionSystemStatus; // 토크 추종 피드백
            out torqueDemand : InternalTorqueDemand;

            @TestOracle {
                requiresCoSimulation = true;
                plantModelDependency = "Engine_Dynamics_Plant_FMU_v2.0";
                description =
                    "정속 주행의 속도 오차(Steady-State Speed Error)는 소프트웨어 로직만으로 판별 불가. 차량/엔진 플랜트 모델 연동(Co-Simulation) 환경에서 측정되어야 하는 오라클입니다.";
            }

            constraint Local_PidSpeedErrorLimit {
                // Before: Local_PidErrorLimit { engineStatus.steadyStateError <= 2.0 }
                CruiseControlLogic::IsCruiseSpeedHeld(
                    validTarget,
                    vehicleSpeed,
                    CruiseControlLogic::cruiseSpeedErrorLimitPct
                )
            }
        }

        // [Step 3: Actuation] 엔진 제어 명령 변환 (수동 가속과 동일한 로직 구조 활용)
        action genEngineCommandOut {
            in torqueReq : InternalTorqueDemand;
            out outAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out outFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_CmdGenLatency { executionTime <= combustionComputeBudget }
        }

        // 상위 블랙박스 제약을 재정의(redefines)하는 assert constraint에도 TestOracle 속성 전파
        @TestOracle {
            requiresCoSimulation = true;
            plantModelDependency = "Engine_Dynamics_Plant_FMU_v2.0";
            description =
                "상위 블랙박스(UF1_2)의 steadyStateErrorLimit 제약을 검증하기 위한 Co-Simulation 오라클";
        }

        assert constraint redefines cruiseSpeedHoldLimit {
            out redefines result = CruiseControlLogic::IsCruiseSpeedHeld(
                targetSpeed,
                dynamics.vehicleSpeed,
                CruiseControlLogic::cruiseSpeedErrorLimitPct
            );
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 (Internal Flows & Bindings) ---

        // 1. 외부 입력 연결
        bind targetSpeed = validateTarget.rawTarget;
        bind dynamics.vehicleSpeed = calcCruiseTorque.vehicleSpeed;
        bind combustionSystemStatus = calcCruiseTorque.engineStatus;

        // 2. 소기능 간 파이프라인 연결
        flow validateTarget.validTarget to calcCruiseTorque.validTarget;
        flow calcCruiseTorque.torqueDemand to genEngineCommandOut.torqueReq;

        // 3. 최종 출력 바인딩
        bind genEngineCommandOut.outAir = airCmd;
        bind genEngineCommandOut.outFuel = fuelCmd;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 3. UF1_3 제동 우선 오버라이드 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF1_3_WB> BrakeOverrideIntervention_WhiteBox :> CF1::CF1_3 {
        private import SafeStateLogic::*;
        in overrideTriggered : Boolean :>> CF1::CF1_3::overrideTriggered;
        out airCmd : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF1::CF1_3::airCmd;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF1::CF1_3::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 구동 요구 중 제동 감지
        action detectBrake {
            in triggered : Boolean;
            out cutoffTrigger : InternalCutoffSignal;

            constraint Local_TriggerGen {
                triggered == true implies cutoffTrigger.isCutoffActive == true
            }

            // 브레이크 센싱 단계의 실행 시간 예산 할당 (4ms)
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_DetectLatency { executionTime <= 3.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        // [Step 2 & 3: Logic & Actuation] 트리거 수신 시 공회전 출력 생성 (Zero-Torque → Idle-Torque)
        action arbitrateOverride {
            in trigger : InternalCutoffSignal;
            out limitedAirOut : IntakeAirFlow; // 구 zeroAirOut
            out limitedFuelOut : FuelIgnitionCommand; // 구 zeroFuelOut

            constraint Local_ForceIdleOutput {
                // 구 Local_ForceZeroOutput
                trigger.isCutoffActive == true implies
                (limitedAirOut.massFlow == idleAirMassFlow and
                    limitedFuelOut.injectionPulseWidth == idleFuelPulseWidth)
            }

            // 긴급 제한 로직 연산 및 액추에이터 제어 명령 하달 단계의 실행 시간 예산 할당 (6ms)
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_OverrideLatency { executionTime <= 3.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        // 10ms 이내 응답 요구(uc1_vc05)를 두 소기능의 지연시간 합산으로 논리적 증명 (기존 그대로)
        assert constraint OverrideLatencyBudget {
            detectBrake.executionTime + arbitrateOverride.executionTime <=
            TimingConceptsAndLimits::overrideStageBudget
        }

        assert constraint redefines SafeStateConstraints {
            out redefines result =
                detectBrake.cutoffTrigger.isCutoffActive == true implies
                IsTorqueLimited(
                    arbitrateOverride.limitedAirOut.massFlow,
                    arbitrateOverride.limitedFuelOut.injectionPulseWidth
                );
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 ---
        bind overrideTriggered = detectBrake.triggered;
        flow detectBrake.cutoffTrigger to arbitrateOverride.trigger;
        bind arbitrateOverride.limitedAirOut = airCmd;
        bind arbitrateOverride.limitedFuelOut = fuelCmd;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 4. Arbiter 중재자 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <Arbiter_WB> SystemArbiter_WhiteBox
        :> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter {
        in cf1_airCmd
            : IntakeAirFlow :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf1_airCmd;
        in cf1_fuelCmd
            : FuelIgnitionCommand
            :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf1_fuelCmd;
        in cf2_airCmd
            : IntakeAirFlow :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf2_airCmd;
        in cf2_fuelCmd
            : FuelIgnitionCommand
            :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf2_fuelCmd;
        in cf3_airCmd
            : IntakeAirFlow :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf3_airCmd;
        in cf3_fuelCmd
            : FuelIgnitionCommand
            :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf3_fuelCmd;

        in cf2_active
            : Boolean :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf2_active;
        in cf3_faultActive
            : Boolean :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::cf3_faultActive;

        out final_airCmd
            : IntakeAirFlow
            :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::final_airCmd;
        out final_fuelCmd
            : FuelIgnitionCommand
            :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::final_fuelCmd;
        out modeChannelBroadcaster
            : ModeChannelData
            :>> FB_ModeArbitrationModel::SystemModeControllerAndArbiter::modeChannelBroadcaster;

        action collectInputs {
            in inCF1_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            in inCF1_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in inCF2_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            in inCF2_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in inCF3_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            in inCF3_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in inCF2_Active : Boolean;
            in inCF3_Fault : Boolean;

            out outCF1_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            out outCF1_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            out outCF2_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            out outCF2_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            out outCF3_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            out outCF3_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            out outCF2_Active : Boolean;
            out outCF3_Fault : Boolean;

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_CollectLatency { executionTime <= 1.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        action evaluatePriority {
            in evalCF1_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            in evalCF1_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in evalCF2_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            in evalCF2_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in evalCF3_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
            in evalCF3_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in evalCF2_Active : Boolean;
            in evalCF3_Fault : Boolean;

            out selectedAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out selectedFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            out selectedMode : SystemOperatingMode;
            out inhibitFlag : Boolean;

            // [논리 분해 1순위] CF3 안전 감시기가 차단(Air=0)을 요구할 경우, 최고 우선순위로 페일세이프(FailSafe) 모드 전환 및 타 기능 억제(Inhibit)
            constraint Local_RouteFailSafe {
                evalCF3_Fault == true implies
                (selectedAir == evalCF3_Air and
                    selectedFuel == evalCF3_Fuel and
                    selectedMode == SystemOperatingMode::FailSafe_Mode and
                    inhibitFlag == true)
            }
            // [논리 분해 2순위] CF3는 정상이나, CF2가 긴급 저감을 요구(현재 요구량 < 정상 주행 요구량)할 경우, 긴급(Emergency) 모드 전환 및 CF1 억제
            constraint Local_RouteEmergency {
                (evalCF3_Fault == false and evalCF2_Active == true) implies
                (selectedMode == SystemOperatingMode::Emergency_Mode and
                    inhibitFlag == false and
                    ((selectedAir == evalCF1_Air and
                            evalCF1_Air.massFlow <= evalCF2_Air.massFlow) or
                        (selectedAir == evalCF2_Air and
                            evalCF2_Air.massFlow < evalCF1_Air.massFlow)) and
                    ((selectedFuel == evalCF1_Fuel and
                            evalCF1_Fuel.injectionPulseWidth <= evalCF2_Fuel.injectionPulseWidth) or
                        (selectedFuel == evalCF2_Fuel and
                            evalCF2_Fuel.injectionPulseWidth < evalCF1_Fuel.injectionPulseWidth)))
            }
            // [논리 분해 3순위] 페일세이프 및 긴급 요구가 없을 시, 기본 상태인 정상 주행(Normal) 모드를 유지하고 CF1 제어 허용
            constraint Local_RouteNormal {
                (evalCF3_Fault == false and evalCF2_Active == false) implies
                (selectedAir == evalCF1_Air and
                    selectedFuel == evalCF1_Fuel and
                    selectedMode == SystemOperatingMode::Normal_Mode and
                    inhibitFlag == false)
            }

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_EvaluateLatency { executionTime <= 1.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        action broadcastOutput {
            in resAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            in resFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in modeStatus : SystemOperatingMode;
            in inhibitStatus : Boolean;

            out finalOutAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out finalOutFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            out finalModeChannel : ModeChannelData;

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_BroadcastLatency { executionTime <= 1.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        assert constraint redefines ArbiterPriorityLogic {
            out redefines result =
                (cf3_faultActive == true implies
                    (modeChannelBroadcaster.currentMode == SystemOperatingMode::FailSafe_Mode and
                        modeChannelBroadcaster.inhibitDrivingControl == true and
                        final_airCmd == cf3_airCmd and
                        final_fuelCmd == cf3_fuelCmd)) and
                ((cf3_faultActive == false and cf2_active == true) implies
                    (modeChannelBroadcaster.currentMode == SystemOperatingMode::Emergency_Mode and
                        modeChannelBroadcaster.inhibitDrivingControl == false and
                        ((final_airCmd == cf1_airCmd and
                                cf1_airCmd.massFlow <= cf2_airCmd.massFlow) or
                            (final_airCmd == cf2_airCmd and
                                cf2_airCmd.massFlow < cf1_airCmd.massFlow)) and
                        ((final_fuelCmd == cf1_fuelCmd and
                                cf1_fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth <=
                                cf2_fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth) or
                            (final_fuelCmd == cf2_fuelCmd and
                                cf2_fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth <
                                cf1_fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth)))) and
                ((cf3_faultActive == false and cf2_active == false) implies
                    (modeChannelBroadcaster.currentMode == SystemOperatingMode::Normal_Mode and
                        modeChannelBroadcaster.inhibitDrivingControl == false and
                        final_airCmd == cf1_airCmd and
                        final_fuelCmd == cf1_fuelCmd));
        }

        assert constraint redefines ArbiterProcessingTime {
            out redefines result =
                collectInputs.executionTime +
                evaluatePriority.executionTime +
                broadcastOutput.executionTime <=
                arbiterBudget;
        }

        bind cf1_airCmd = collectInputs.inCF1_Air;
        bind cf1_fuelCmd = collectInputs.inCF1_Fuel;
        bind cf2_airCmd = collectInputs.inCF2_Air;
        bind cf2_fuelCmd = collectInputs.inCF2_Fuel;
        bind cf3_airCmd = collectInputs.inCF3_Air;
        bind cf3_fuelCmd = collectInputs.inCF3_Fuel;

        flow collectInputs.outCF1_Air to evaluatePriority.evalCF1_Air;
        flow collectInputs.outCF1_Fuel to evaluatePriority.evalCF1_Fuel;
        flow collectInputs.outCF2_Air to evaluatePriority.evalCF2_Air;
        flow collectInputs.outCF2_Fuel to evaluatePriority.evalCF2_Fuel;
        flow collectInputs.outCF3_Air to evaluatePriority.evalCF3_Air;
        flow collectInputs.outCF3_Fuel to evaluatePriority.evalCF3_Fuel;

        flow evaluatePriority.selectedAir to broadcastOutput.resAir;
        flow evaluatePriority.selectedFuel to broadcastOutput.resFuel;
        flow evaluatePriority.selectedMode to broadcastOutput.modeStatus;
        flow evaluatePriority.inhibitFlag to broadcastOutput.inhibitStatus;

        bind broadcastOutput.finalOutAir = final_airCmd;
        bind broadcastOutput.finalOutFuel = final_fuelCmd;
        bind broadcastOutput.finalModeChannel = modeChannelBroadcaster;

        bind cf2_active = collectInputs.inCF2_Active;
        bind cf3_faultActive = collectInputs.inCF3_Fault;
        flow collectInputs.outCF2_Active to evaluatePriority.evalCF2_Active;
        flow collectInputs.outCF3_Fault to evaluatePriority.evalCF3_Fault;
    }
} // package FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel

##02. Functional Viewpoint/3-2. FW UF2 WhiteBox Model.sysml##
package FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel {
    doc
    /* [CF2 긴급 토크 개입 - 화이트박스 모델 파트 2]
     * 외부 능동안전 제어기(ESP/TCU)의 개입 요구, 통신 지연, 액추에이터 고착 등
     * 긴급 상황에 대응하는 UF2_1, UF2_2, UF2_3의 상세 내부 구조와 제약 할당을 명세합니다.
     */

    private import FB_FunctionalBlackBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import RV_InterfaceModel::*;
    private import ReqCommon::*;
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SI::*;

    @MetaInformation {
        version = "v0.1";
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 0. CF2 전용 내부 공용 데이터 타입
    // ==========================================
    item def InternalDelaySignal {
        attribute isDelayed : Boolean;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 1. UF2_1 병렬 토크 저감 제어 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF2_1_WB> ParallelTorqueReduction_WhiteBox :> CF2::CF2_1 {
        in torqueIntervention : TorqueInterventionRequest :>> CF2::CF2_1::torqueIntervention;
        out airCmd : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF2::CF2_1::airIntake;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF2::CF2_1::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 개입 요청 수신 및 검증
        action receiveInterventionReq {
            in rawReq : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            out validReq : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // [로컬 제약] 외부 제어기의 긴급 개입 요구 수신 및 유효성 검증 레이턴시 (예산 5ms)
            constraint Local_ValidationLatency { executionTime <= 2.0 [milli * s] }
            // [로컬 제약] 토크 델타가 음수(-, 저감)일 때만 유효한 개입 요청으로 취급하는 선행 조건
            constraint Local_ValidIntervention { rawReq.requestedTorqueDelta < 0.0 [N * m] }
        }

        // [Step 2: Core Logic] 공기/연료 저감량 동기화 연산
        action calcReductionTorque {
            in validReq : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            out reducedTorque : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::InternalTorqueDemand;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // [로컬 제약] 공기량 및 연료 분사량 동기화 축소 알고리즘 연산 레이턴시 (예산 5ms)
            constraint Local_CalculationLatency { executionTime <= 3.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        // [Step 3: Actuation] 실제 액추에이터 출력 제한 명령 생성
        action limitActuatorOutput {
            in targetTorque : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::InternalTorqueDemand;
            out outAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out outFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // [로컬 제약] 출력 클램핑(Clamping) 및 액추에이터 명령 생성 레이턴시 (예산 5ms)
            constraint Local_CmdGenLatency { executionTime <= 3.0 [milli * s] }
            // [로컬 제약] 이전 사이클 대비 현재 사이클의 공기량 및 연료량이 반드시 감소해야 한다는 물리적 저감 강제 로직
            constraint Local_EnforceReduction {
                outAir.massFlow < outAir.previousMassFlow and
                outFuel.injectionPulseWidth < outFuel.previousPulseWidth
            }
        }

        attribute pipelineLatency : TimeValue =
            receiveInterventionReq.executionTime +
            calcReductionTorque.executionTime +
            limitActuatorOutput.executionTime;

        assert constraint redefines InterventionLatency {
            out redefines result =
                airCmd.responseTime == pipelineLatency and
                fuelCmd.responseTime == pipelineLatency and
                pipelineLatency <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::interventionStageBudget;
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 ---
        bind torqueIntervention = receiveInterventionReq.rawReq;

        flow receiveInterventionReq.validReq to calcReductionTorque.validReq;
        flow calcReductionTorque.reducedTorque to limitActuatorOutput.targetTorque;

        bind limitActuatorOutput.outAir = airCmd;
        bind limitActuatorOutput.outFuel = fuelCmd;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 2. UF2_2 통신 지연 보상 제어 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF2_2_WB> CommunicationDelayCompensation_WhiteBox :> CF2::CF2_2 {
        in torqueIntervention : TorqueInterventionRequest :>> CF2::CF2_2::torqueIntervention;
        out airIntake : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF2::CF2_2::airIntake;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF2::CF2_2::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 통신 레이턴시 모니터링
        action monitorMessageAge {
            in req : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            out delaySignal : InternalDelaySignal;
            constraint Local_DetectDelay {
                (req.messageAge > TimingConceptsAndLimits::commDelayThreshold and
                    req.messageAge <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::commTimeout) implies //
                delaySignal.isDelayed == true
            }
        }

        // [Step 2 & 3: Logic & Actuation] 이전 출력 상태 유지 (Hold) 로직
        action holdPreviousState {
            in trigger : InternalDelaySignal;
            out holdAirOut : IntakeAirFlow;
            out holdFuelOut : FuelIgnitionCommand;

            // [논리 분해 2단계: Logic/Actuation] 지연 플래그 수신 시, 새로운 제어값 연산을 멈추고 이전 사이클의 유효 출력값(Hold)을 유지
            constraint Local_FreezeOutput {
                trigger.isDelayed == true implies
                (holdAirOut.massFlow == holdAirOut.previousMassFlow and
                    holdFuelOut.injectionPulseWidth == holdFuelOut.previousPulseWidth)
            }
        }

        assert constraint redefines DelayCompensation {
            out redefines result =
                monitorMessageAge.delaySignal.isDelayed == true implies
                (holdPreviousState.holdAirOut.massFlow ==
                    holdPreviousState.holdAirOut.previousMassFlow and
                    holdPreviousState.holdFuelOut.injectionPulseWidth ==
                    holdPreviousState.holdFuelOut.previousPulseWidth);
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 ---
        bind torqueIntervention = monitorMessageAge.req;
        flow monitorMessageAge.delaySignal to holdPreviousState.trigger;
        bind holdPreviousState.holdAirOut = airIntake;
        bind holdPreviousState.holdFuelOut = fuelCmd;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 3. UF2_3 고착 대응 연료 차단 제어 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF2_3_WB> ActuatorStuckFuelCut_WhiteBox :> CF2::CF2_3 {
        in stuckConfirmed : Boolean :>> CF2::CF2_3::stuckConfirmed;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF2::CF2_3::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 고착 확정 플래그를 내부 차단 신호로 변환 (판정 자체는 FE 디바운스가 담당)
        action detectActuatorStuck {
            in confirmed : Boolean;
            out stuckTrigger : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::InternalCutoffSignal;

            // [논리 분해 1단계: Sensing] 스로틀 저감 명령에도 불구하고 실제 피드백 공기량이 줄어들지 않으면(고착 상태) 차단 플래그 활성화
            constraint Local_DetectStuck {
                confirmed == true implies stuckTrigger.isCutoffActive == true
            }

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_DetectLatency { executionTime <= 2.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        // [Step 2 & 3: Logic & Actuation] 연료 차단 컷오프 실행
        action executeEmergencyFuelCut {
            in trigger : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::InternalCutoffSignal;
            out zeroFuelOut : FuelIgnitionCommand;

            // [논리 분해 2단계: Actuation] 공기 유입 통제 불가 상황이므로, 최종 안전 상태(Safe State)인 연료 차단 강제 실행
            constraint Local_FuelCut {
                trigger.isCutoffActive == true implies zeroFuelOut.injectionPulseWidth == 0.0 [s]
            }

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_CutLatency { executionTime <= 3.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        assert constraint redefines StuckFailSafe {
            out redefines result =
                stuckConfirmed == true implies
                executeEmergencyFuelCut.zeroFuelOut.injectionPulseWidth == 0.0 [s];
        }

        assert constraint redefines StuckResponseTime {
            out redefines result =
                stuckConfirmed == true implies
                detectActuatorStuck.executionTime + executeEmergencyFuelCut.executionTime <=
                TimingConceptsAndLimits::stuckCutStageBudget;
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 ---
        bind stuckConfirmed = detectActuatorStuck.confirmed;
        flow detectActuatorStuck.stuckTrigger to executeEmergencyFuelCut.trigger;
        bind executeEmergencyFuelCut.zeroFuelOut = fuelCmd;
    }
    // ==========================================
    // 4. UF2_4 긴급 개입 해제 및 제어 복귀 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF2_4_WB> InterventionReleaseControl_WhiteBox :> CF2::CF2_4 {
        in torqueIntervention : TorqueInterventionRequest :>> CF2::CF2_4::torqueIntervention;
        out airIntake : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF2::CF2_4::airIntake;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF2::CF2_4::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 개입 해제 요청 수신
        action detectReleaseReq {
            in req : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            out isReleased : Boolean;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // [논리 분해 1단계] 해제 요청(delta >= 0) 감지
            constraint Local_DetectRelease {
                req.requestedTorqueDelta >= 0.0 [N * m] implies isReleased == true
            }
        }

        // [Step 2 & 3: Logic & Actuation] 제어권 복귀 처리
        action executeRelease {
            in releaseFlag : Boolean;
            out outAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out outFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            // Arbiter가 CF1(운전자 요구)으로 모드(Normal_Mode)를 원활히 전환할 수 있도록,
            // 개입 해제 상태일 때는 CF2 측에서의 추가적인 출력 강제 제한을 해제함
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 ---
        bind torqueIntervention = detectReleaseReq.req;
        flow detectReleaseReq.isReleased to executeRelease.releaseFlag;

        bind executeRelease.outAir = airIntake;
        bind executeRelease.outFuel = fuelCmd;
    }

    action def <UF2_5_WB> InterventionRequestIntegrityGuard_WhiteBox :> CF2::CF2_5 {
        in torqueIntervention : TorqueInterventionRequest :>> CF2::CF2_5::torqueIntervention;
        in integrityError : Boolean :>> CF2::CF2_5::integrityError;
        out validatedRequest : TorqueInterventionRequest :>> CF2::CF2_5::validatedRequest;

        action checkFrame {
            in req : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            in error : Boolean;
            out passed : Boolean;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_Check { passed == (error == false) }
            constraint Local_CheckLatency { executionTime <= 1.0 [milli * s] }
        }
        action gate {
            in req : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            in passed : Boolean;
            out validated : TorqueInterventionRequest;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_Gate {
                (passed implies validated == req) and
                (not passed implies validated.requestedTorqueDelta == 0.0 [N * m])
            }
            constraint Local_GateLatency { executionTime <= 1.0 [milli * s] }
        }
        assert constraint redefines IntegrityGuardLatency {
            out redefines result =
                checkFrame.executionTime + gate.executionTime <=
                TimingConceptsAndLimits::integrityGuardBudget;
        }
        assert constraint redefines IntegrityGuard {
            out redefines result =
                integrityError == true implies validatedRequest.requestedTorqueDelta == 0.0 [N * m];
        }
        bind torqueIntervention = checkFrame.req;
        bind integrityError = checkFrame.error;
        bind torqueIntervention = gate.req;
        flow checkFrame.passed to gate.passed;
        bind gate.validated = validatedRequest;
    }
} // package FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel

##02. Functional Viewpoint/3-3. FW UF3 WhiteBox Model.sysml##
package FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel {
    doc
    /* [CF3 하드웨어 안전 모니터링 - 화이트박스 모델 파트 ]
     * 엔진 마찰/지연 실시간 보정(UF3_1)과 치명적 결함 페일세이프(UF3_2)의 
     * 상세 내부 구조, 내부 트리거 논리, 그리고 처리 시간 할당을 명세합니다.
     */

    private import FB_FunctionalBlackBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import RV_InterfaceModel::*;
    private import ReqCommon::*;
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import SafeStateLogic::*;

    @MetaInformation {
        version = "v0.1";
    }
    // ==========================================
    // 0. CF3 전용 내부 공용 데이터 타입
    // ==========================================
    item def InternalFatalTrigger {
        attribute isFatalActive : Boolean;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 1. UF3_1 하드웨어 마찰 및 지연 보정 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF3_1_WB> HardwareDelayAndFrictionCompensation_WhiteBox :> CF3::CF3_1 {
        in baseAirCmd : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF3::CF3_1::baseAirCmd; // [신규]
        in baseFuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF3::CF3_1::baseFuelCmd; // [신규]
        in sensor : EngineSensorData :>> CF3::CF3_1::sensor;
        in combustionStatus : CombustionSystemStatus :>> CF3::CF3_1::combustionStatus;
        out airIntake : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF3::CF3_1::airIntake;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF3::CF3_1::fuelCmd;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 변경 없음
        action monitorSensorError {
            in currentSensor : EngineSensorData;
            in currentCombustion : CombustionSystemStatus;
            out errorEst : InternalErrorData;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_SensingLatency { executionTime <= 2.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        // [Step 2: Core Logic] 보상값 연산 (과열 게이팅 삭제, 보정량 한계 추가)
        action calcCorrectionValue {
            in rawError : InternalErrorData;
            in sensorCtx : EngineSensorData; // Before: tempCheck
            out correctionDemand : InternalCorrectionCmd;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            constraint Local_CalcLatency { executionTime <= 2.0 [milli * s] }
            constraint Local_CorrectionBound {
                // [신규] 보정량은 한계 이내
                correctionDemand.targetAirAdjust >= -maxAirCorrection and
                correctionDemand.targetAirAdjust <= maxAirCorrection and
                correctionDemand.targetFuelAdjust >= -maxFuelCorrection and
                correctionDemand.targetFuelAdjust <= maxFuelCorrection
            }
        }

        // [Step 3: Actuation] 기준 명령에 보정량을 가감 (직렬 후처리)
        action applyCorrection {
            in baseAir : IntakeAirFlow; // [신규]
            in baseFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand; // [신규]
            in correctionReq : InternalCorrectionCmd;
            out adjAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out adjFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;

            constraint Local_ActuationLatency { executionTime <= 2.0 [milli * s] }
            constraint Local_SeriesApply {
                // [신규] 기준 + 보정량
                adjAir.massFlow == baseAir.massFlow + correctionReq.targetAirAdjust and
                adjFuel.injectionPulseWidth ==
                baseFuel.injectionPulseWidth + correctionReq.targetFuelAdjust
            }
        }

        assert constraint redefines ResponseTimeConstraint {
            out redefines result =
                monitorSensorError.executionTime +
                calcCorrectionValue.executionTime +
                applyCorrection.executionTime <=
                compensationBudget;
        }

        assert constraint redefines BoundedCorrection { // BB 제약과 같은 본문 (S2와 같은 방식)
            out redefines result =
                airIntake.massFlow >= baseAirCmd.massFlow - maxAirCorrection and
                airIntake.massFlow <= baseAirCmd.massFlow + maxAirCorrection and
                fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth >=
                baseFuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth - maxFuelCorrection and
                fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth <= baseFuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth + maxFuelCorrection;
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 ---
        bind sensor = monitorSensorError.currentSensor;
        bind sensor = calcCorrectionValue.sensorCtx; // Before: tempCheck
        bind combustionStatus = monitorSensorError.currentCombustion;
        bind baseAirCmd = applyCorrection.baseAir; // [신규]
        bind baseFuelCmd = applyCorrection.baseFuel; // [신규]

        flow monitorSensorError.errorEst to calcCorrectionValue.rawError;
        flow calcCorrectionValue.correctionDemand to applyCorrection.correctionReq;

        bind applyCorrection.adjAir = airIntake;
        bind applyCorrection.adjFuel = fuelCmd;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 2. UF3_2 치명적 센서 결함 페일세이프 화이트박스 분해
    // ==========================================
    action def <UF3_2_WB> CriticalSensorFaultFailSafe_WhiteBox :> CF3::CF3_2 {
        in faultCause : FaultCause :>> CF3::CF3_2::faultCause;
        out airIntake : IntakeAirFlow :>> CF3::CF3_2::airIntake;
        out fuelCmd : FuelIgnitionCommand :>> CF3::CF3_2::fuelCmd;
        out dashboard : DashboardWarning :>> CF3::CF3_2::dashboard;

        // [Step 1: Sensing] 확정된 고장 원인을 내부 차단 플래그로 변환 (확정 판정 자체는 FE가 담당)
        action classifyFault {
            // Before: checkCriticalLimits
            in cause : FaultCause;
            out fatalFaultTrigger : InternalFatalTrigger;
            constraint Local_DetectFatal {
                cause != FaultCause::NoFault implies fatalFaultTrigger.isFatalActive == true
            }

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_ClassifyLatency { executionTime <= 2.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        // [Step 2 & 3: Logic & Actuation] 페일세이프 실행 및 시스템 셧다운
        action shutdownSystem {
            in trigger : InternalFatalTrigger;
            out cutAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            out cutFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            out warnSign : DashboardWarning;

            // [논리 분해 2단계: Actuation] 차단 플래그 수신 시 즉시 공기/연료 제로 클램핑 및 클러스터에 치명적 결함(Critical) 경고 점등
            constraint Local_ExecuteShutdown {
                trigger.isFatalActive == true implies
                (SafeStateLogic::IsFuelCutOff(fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth) and
                    warnSign.status == DashboardWarningStatusEnum::Critical)
            }

            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_ShutdownLatency { executionTime <= 3.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        assert constraint redefines FatalFaultCutoff {
            out redefines result =
                classifyFault.fatalFaultTrigger.isFatalActive == true implies
                (shutdownSystem.cutAir.massFlow <= 0.0 [kg / s] and
                    shutdownSystem.cutFuel.injectionPulseWidth <= 0.0 [s] and
                    shutdownSystem.warnSign.status == DashboardWarningStatusEnum::Critical);
        }

        assert constraint redefines FatalFaultResponseTime {
            out redefines result =
                faultCause != FaultCause::NoFault implies
                classifyFault.executionTime + shutdownSystem.executionTime <=
                TimingConceptsAndLimits::failSafeStageBudget;
        }

        // --- 내부 데이터 흐름 및 포트 바인딩 ---
        bind faultCause = classifyFault.cause;
        flow classifyFault.fatalFaultTrigger to shutdownSystem.trigger;

        bind shutdownSystem.cutAir = airIntake;
        bind shutdownSystem.cutFuel = fuelCmd;
        bind shutdownSystem.warnSign = dashboard;
    }

    action def <UF3_3_WB> RedundantSensorDeviationMonitoring_WhiteBox :> CF3::CF3_3 {
        in pedalPosition : AccelerationPedalPosition :>> CF3::CF3_3::pedalPosition;
        in throttleFeedback : ThrottlePositionFeedback :>> CF3::CF3_3::throttleFeedback;
        out pedalFaultFlag : Boolean :>> CF3::CF3_3::pedalFaultFlag;
        out throttleFaultFlag : Boolean :>> CF3::CF3_3::throttleFaultFlag;

        action comparePedalChannels {
            in pos : AccelerationPedalPosition;
            out flag : Boolean;
            constraint Local_Pedal {
                flag ==
                IsChannelDeviated(pos.pvs1Percentage, pos.pvs2Percentage, redundancyDeviationLimit)
            }
        }
        action compareThrottleChannels {
            in fb : ThrottlePositionFeedback;
            out flag : Boolean;
            constraint Local_Throttle {
                flag ==
                IsChannelDeviated(fb.tps1Percentage, fb.tps2Percentage, redundancyDeviationLimit)
            }
        }
        bind pedalPosition = comparePedalChannels.pos;
        bind comparePedalChannels.flag = pedalFaultFlag;
        bind throttleFeedback = compareThrottleChannels.fb;
        bind compareThrottleChannels.flag = throttleFaultFlag;
    }

    action def <UF3_4_WB> ThrottleTrackingMonitoring_WhiteBox :> CF3::CF3_4 {
        in throttleCmd : ThrottlePositionCommand :>> CF3::CF3_4::throttleCmd;
        in throttleFeedback : ThrottlePositionFeedback :>> CF3::CF3_4::throttleFeedback;
        out trackingFaultFlag : Boolean :>> CF3::CF3_4::trackingFaultFlag;

        action compareTracking {
            in cmd : ThrottlePositionCommand;
            in fb : ThrottlePositionFeedback;
            out flag : Boolean;
            constraint Local_Tracking {
                flag ==
                ThrottleStuckLogic::IsThrottleDeviated(
                    cmd.openingPercentage,
                    fb.openingPercentage,
                    ThrottleStuckLogic::stuckDeviationLimit
                )
            }
        }

        bind throttleCmd = compareTracking.cmd;
        bind throttleFeedback = compareTracking.fb;
        bind compareTracking.flag = trackingFaultFlag;
    }
} // package FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel

##02. Functional Viewpoint/3-4. FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel.sysml##
package FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel {
    doc
    /* 캘리브레이션 및 진단 기능의 상세 화이트박스 동작을 명세합니다.
     */

    private import FB_FunctionalBlackBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import RV_InterfaceModel::*;
    private import ReqCommon::*;
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import SafeStateLogic::*;
    private import DtcStroage::*;

    @MetaInformation {
        version = "v0.1";
    }

    action def <UF4_1_WB> MemoryAccessAuthorization_WhiteBox :> CF4::CF4_1 {
        in calibration_Req : CalibrationRequest :>> CF4::CF4_1::calibration_Req;
        in expectedCredential : Integer :>> CF4::CF4_1::expectedCredential;
        in ignition : IgnitionState :>> CF4::CF4_1::ignition;
        in vehicleSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue :>> CF4::CF4_1::vehicleSpeed;
        out isAuthorized : Boolean :>> CF4::CF4_1::isAuthorized;
        out authStatus : CalibrationStatus :>> CF4::CF4_1::authStatus;

        action evaluateAuthorization {
            in req : CalibrationRequest;
            in credential : Integer;
            in currentIgnition : IgnitionState;
            in currentSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;
            out authorized : Boolean;
            constraint Local_Authorization {
                authorized ==
                IsCalibrationAuthorized(
                    req.targetMemoryAddress,
                    req.accessCredential,
                    credential,
                    req.operation,
                    currentIgnition.status,
                    currentSpeed
                )
            }
        }

        assert constraint redefines AuthDefinition {
            out redefines result =
                isAuthorized ==
                IsCalibrationAuthorized(
                    calibration_Req.targetMemoryAddress,
                    calibration_Req.accessCredential,
                    expectedCredential,
                    calibration_Req.operation,
                    ignition.status,
                    vehicleSpeed
                );
        }

        bind calibration_Req = evaluateAuthorization.req;
        bind expectedCredential = evaluateAuthorization.credential;
        bind ignition = evaluateAuthorization.currentIgnition;
        bind vehicleSpeed = evaluateAuthorization.currentSpeed;
        bind evaluateAuthorization.authorized = isAuthorized;
    }

    action def <UF4_2_WB> DataIntegrityVerification_WhiteBox :> CF4::CF4_2 {
        in calibration_Req : CalibrationRequest :>> CF4::CF4_2::calibration_Req;
        in isAuthorized : Boolean :>> CF4::CF4_2::isAuthorized;
        out isIntegrityOk : Boolean :>> CF4::CF4_2::isIntegrityOk;
        out verifyStatus : CalibrationStatus :>> CF4::CF4_2::verifyStatus;

        action computeChecksum {
            in req : CalibrationRequest;
            out computed : Integer;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_ComputeLatency { executionTime <= 60.0 [milli * s] }
        }
        action compareAndCommit {
            in req : CalibrationRequest;
            in computed : Integer;
            out ok : Boolean;
            out status : CalibrationStatus;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_Compare { ok == (computed == req.payloadChecksum) }
            constraint Local_Response {
                ok implies (status.responseCode == 200 and status.rolledBack == false)
            }
            constraint Local_CommitLatency { executionTime <= 20.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        assert constraint redefines IntegrityCheck {
            out redefines result =
                (isAuthorized and
                    calibration_Req.operation == CalibrationOperationEnum::Write) implies
                (isIntegrityOk ==
                    (verifyStatus.verifiedChecksum == calibration_Req.payloadChecksum) and
                    (isIntegrityOk implies
                        (verifyStatus.responseCode == 200 and
                            verifyStatus.rolledBack == false and
                            computeChecksum.executionTime + compareAndCommit.executionTime <=
                            TimingConceptsAndLimits::mapUpdateBudget)));
        }

        bind calibration_Req = computeChecksum.req;
        bind calibration_Req = compareAndCommit.req;
        flow computeChecksum.computed to compareAndCommit.computed;
        bind compareAndCommit.ok = isIntegrityOk;
        bind compareAndCommit.status = verifyStatus;
    }

    action def <UF4_3_WB> InvalidAccessRejectionAndRollback_WhiteBox :> CF4::CF4_3 {
        in calibration_Req : CalibrationRequest :>> CF4::CF4_3::calibration_Req;
        in isAuthorized : Boolean :>> CF4::CF4_3::isAuthorized;
        in integrityFailed : Boolean :>> CF4::CF4_3::integrityFailed;
        out errorStatus : CalibrationStatus :>> CF4::CF4_3::errorStatus;

        action rejectOrRollback {
            in req : CalibrationRequest;
            in authorized : Boolean;
            in failedIntegrity : Boolean;
            out status : CalibrationStatus;
            constraint Local_UnauthorizedRejection {
                not authorized implies
                (status.responseCode != 200 and
                    status.rolledBack == false and
                    status.responseTime <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::addressRejectBudget)
            }
            constraint Local_IntegrityRollback {
                failedIntegrity implies
                (status.responseCode != 200 and
                    status.rolledBack == true and
                    status.responseTime <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::mapUpdateBudget)
            }
        }

        assert constraint redefines UnauthorizedRejection {
            out redefines result =
                not isAuthorized implies
                (errorStatus.responseCode != 200 and
                    errorStatus.rolledBack == false and
                    errorStatus.responseTime <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::addressRejectBudget);
        }

        assert constraint redefines IntegrityRollback {
            out redefines result =
                integrityFailed implies
                (errorStatus.responseCode != 200 and
                    errorStatus.rolledBack == true and
                    errorStatus.responseTime <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::mapUpdateBudget);
        }

        bind calibration_Req = rejectOrRollback.req;
        bind isAuthorized = rejectOrRollback.authorized;
        bind integrityFailed = rejectOrRollback.failedIntegrity;
        bind rejectOrRollback.status = errorStatus;
    }

    action def <UF5_1_WB> DiagnosticTroubleCodeRead_WhiteBox :> CF5::CF5_1 {
        in diag_Req : DiagnosticRequest :>> CF5::CF5_1::diag_Req;
        in faultMemory : FaultMemoryRecord :>> CF5::CF5_1::faultMemory;
        out dtcStatus : DiagnosticStatus :>> CF5::CF5_1::dtcStatus;

        action lookupFaultMemory {
            in memory : FaultMemoryRecord;
            out foundCode : Integer;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_LookupLatency { executionTime <= 20.0 [milli * s] }
            constraint Local_Lookup { foundCode == memory.dtcCode }
        }
        action buildDtcResponse {
            in code : Integer;
            out status : DiagnosticStatus;
            attribute executionTime : TimeValue;
            constraint Local_Response { status.responseCode == 200 and status.dtcCode == code }
            constraint Local_BuildLatency { executionTime <= 20.0 [milli * s] }
        }

        assert constraint redefines DtcResponse {
            out redefines result =
                diag_Req.serviceId == DtcStroage::diagServiceReadDtc implies
                (dtcStatus.responseCode == 200 and
                    dtcStatus.dtcCode == faultMemory.dtcCode and
                    lookupFaultMemory.executionTime + buildDtcResponse.executionTime <=
                    TimingConceptsAndLimits::dtcResponseBudget);
        }

        bind faultMemory = lookupFaultMemory.memory;
        flow lookupFaultMemory.foundCode to buildDtcResponse.code;
        bind buildDtcResponse.status = dtcStatus;
    }

    action def <UF5_2_WB> ZeroPointCalibrationRoutine_WhiteBox :> CF5::CF5_2 {
        in diag_Req : DiagnosticRequest :>> CF5::CF5_2::diag_Req;
        in ignition : IgnitionState :>> CF5::CF5_2::ignition;
        in vehicleSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue :>> CF5::CF5_2::vehicleSpeed;
        out zeroStatus : DiagnosticStatus :>> CF5::CF5_2::zeroStatus;

        action checkServiceMode {
            in currentIgnition : IgnitionState;
            in currentSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;
            out allowed : Boolean;
            constraint Local_ServiceMode {
                allowed == IsServiceModeAllowed(currentIgnition.status, currentSpeed)
            }
        }
        action executeZeroPointRoutine {
            in req : DiagnosticRequest;
            in serviceModeAllowed : Boolean;
            out status : DiagnosticStatus;
            constraint Local_Completion {
                (req.serviceId == diagServiceRoutineControl and serviceModeAllowed) implies
                (status.responseCode == 200 and
                    status.responseTime <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::zeroPointBudget)
            }
        }

        assert constraint redefines ZeroPointCal {
            out redefines result =
                (diag_Req.serviceId == diagServiceRoutineControl and
                    IsServiceModeAllowed(ignition.status, vehicleSpeed)) implies
                (zeroStatus.responseCode == 200 and
                    zeroStatus.responseTime <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::zeroPointBudget);
        }

        bind ignition = checkServiceMode.currentIgnition;
        bind vehicleSpeed = checkServiceMode.currentSpeed;
        bind diag_Req = executeZeroPointRoutine.req;
        flow checkServiceMode.allowed to executeZeroPointRoutine.serviceModeAllowed;
        bind executeZeroPointRoutine.status = zeroStatus;
    }

    action def <UF5_4_WB> FaultMemoryStorage_WhiteBox :> CF5::CF5_4 {
        in confirmedFaultCause : FaultCause :>> CF5::CF5_4::confirmedFaultCause;
        out dtcRecord : FaultMemoryRecord :>> CF5::CF5_4::dtcRecord;

        action encodeFaultCause {
            in cause : FaultCause;
            out code : Integer;
            constraint Local_Encode {
                cause != FaultCause::NoFault implies code > 0
            }
        }
        action persistFaultRecord {
            in cause : FaultCause;
            in code : Integer;
            out record : FaultMemoryRecord;
            constraint Local_Persist {
                record.dtcCode == code and
                (cause != FaultCause::NoFault implies
                    record.storageTime <= DtcStroage::dtcStorageBudget)
            }
        }

        assert constraint redefines StorageConstraint {
            out redefines result =
                confirmedFaultCause != FaultCause::NoFault implies
                (dtcRecord.dtcCode > 0 and
                    dtcRecord.storageTime <= DtcStroage::dtcStorageBudget);
        }

        bind confirmedFaultCause = encodeFaultCause.cause;
        bind confirmedFaultCause = persistFaultRecord.cause;
        flow encodeFaultCause.code to persistFaultRecord.code;
        bind persistFaultRecord.record = dtcRecord;
    }
} // package FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel

##02. Functional Viewpoint/4-1. FV Functional Equivalence Verification Model .sysml##
package FV_FunctionalEquivalenceVerificationModel {
    doc
    /* [기능 동등성 검증 모델 (Functional Equivalence Verification Model)]
     * 본 패키지는 블랙박스 기능의 제약을 화이트박스 구현이 만족하는지 정제(refinement)하거나,
     * 출력 등가성이 요구되는 기능의 입출력을 비교하여 검증합니다.
     * 수정 사항: VerdictKind 및 PassIf 등 SysML v2 검증 시맨틱에 맞춘 구조 전면 리팩토링
     */

    private import FB_FunctionalBlackBoxModel::*;
    private import FB_ModeArbitrationModel::*;
    private import FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import RV_ContextModel::*;
    private import RV_InterfaceModel::*;
    private import VerificationCases::*;
    private import ReqCommon::*;
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SI::*;

    @MetaInformation {
        version = "v0.1";
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. CF1 (주행 토크 제어 및 중재 기능) 정제 검증
    // =========================================================================

    // 1.1 수동 가속 제어 (UF1_1)
    action def UF1_1_RefinementTestHarness {
        in test_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in test_accel : AccelerationIntent;
        in test_normalDriving : Boolean;
        in test_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus; // 관측 입력 (판정에는 미사용)
        in test_dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback;

        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::ManualAccelerationControl_WhiteBox {
            in ignition = test_ignition;
            in accel = test_accel;
            in combustionSystemStatus = test_combustion;
            in dynamics = test_dynamics;
            in normalDriving = test_normalDriving;
        }

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            (test_ignition.status == IgnitionStatusEnum::On and
                test_accel.pedalDisplacement > 0.0 [mm]) implies
            (wb.filterPedalInput.executionTime +
                wb.calcManualTorque.executionTime +
                wb.genEngineCommand.executionTime <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::cf1PathBudget);

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_CF1_1 {
        subject cF1_1_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF1_1_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in v_accel : AccelerationIntent;
        in v_normalDriving : Boolean;
        in v_test_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;
        in v_test_dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-13';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-08';
        }

        action harness : UF1_1_RefinementTestHarness {
            in test_ignition = v_ignition;
            in test_accel = v_accel;
            in test_normalDriving = v_normalDriving;
            in test_combustion = v_test_combustion;
            in test_dynamics = v_test_dynamics;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // 1.2 정속 주행 제어 (UF1_2)
    action def UF1_2_RefinementTestHarness {
        in test_targetSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;
        in test_dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback;
        in test_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::CruiseControlDriving_WhiteBox {
            in targetSpeed = test_targetSpeed;
            in dynamics = test_dynamics;
            in combustionSystemStatus = test_combustion;
        }

        @TestOracle {
            requiresCoSimulation = true;
            plantModelDependency = "Engine_Dynamics_Plant_FMU_v2.0";
            description = "정속 주행 속도 유지 한계는 차량 플랜트 연동 관측 오라클로 판정합니다.";
        }
        out isRefined : Boolean =
            (test_targetSpeed > 0.0 [km / h]) implies
            (CruiseControlLogic::IsCruiseSpeedHeld(
                    test_targetSpeed,
                    test_dynamics.vehicleSpeed,
                    CruiseControlLogic::cruiseSpeedErrorLimitPct
                ) and
                wb.genEngineCommandOut.executionTime <=
                    TimingConceptsAndLimits::combustionComputeBudget);

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_CF1_2 {
        subject cF1_2_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF1_2_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_targetSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;
        in v_dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback;
        in v_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-23';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-08';
        }

        action harness : UF1_2_RefinementTestHarness {
            in test_targetSpeed = v_targetSpeed;
            in test_dynamics = v_dynamics;
            in test_combustion = v_combustion;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // 1.3 제동 우선 오버라이드 제어 (UF1_3)
    action def CF1_3_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        in test_overrideTriggered : Boolean;

        // BB는 상한(L1)만 규정하므로 값 일치가 아니라 정제(refinement) 판정: WB 출력이 BB의 SafeStateConstraints를 만족하는가

        out isEquivalent : Boolean =
            test_overrideTriggered implies
            SafeStateLogic::IsTorqueLimited(wb.airCmd.massFlow, wb.fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth);

        action bb : CF1::CF1_3 { in overrideTriggered = test_overrideTriggered; }
        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::BrakeOverrideIntervention_WhiteBox {
            in overrideTriggered = test_overrideTriggered;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_CF1_3 {
        subject cF1_3_EquivalenceTestHarness : CF1_3_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_overrideTriggered : Boolean;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-01a';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-01b';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-10';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-20';
        }
        action harness : CF1_3_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_overrideTriggered = v_overrideTriggered;
        }
        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // 1.4 시스템 모드 중재자 (Arbiter)
    action def Arbiter_RefinementTestHarness {
        in t_cf1_air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in t_cf1_fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in t_cf2_air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in t_cf2_fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in t_cf3_air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in t_cf3_fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in t_cf2_active : Boolean;
        in t_cf3_fault : Boolean;

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            (t_cf3_fault implies
                (wb.modeChannelBroadcaster.currentMode == SystemOperatingMode::FailSafe_Mode and
                    wb.modeChannelBroadcaster.inhibitDrivingControl and
                    wb.final_airCmd == t_cf3_air and
                    wb.final_fuelCmd == t_cf3_fuel)) and
            ((not t_cf3_fault and t_cf2_active) implies
                (wb.modeChannelBroadcaster.currentMode == SystemOperatingMode::Emergency_Mode and
                    not wb.modeChannelBroadcaster.inhibitDrivingControl and
                    ((wb.final_airCmd == t_cf1_air and t_cf1_air.massFlow <= t_cf2_air.massFlow) or
                        (wb.final_airCmd == t_cf2_air and t_cf2_air.massFlow < t_cf1_air.massFlow)) and
                    ((wb.final_fuelCmd == t_cf1_fuel and
                            t_cf1_fuel.injectionPulseWidth <= t_cf2_fuel.injectionPulseWidth) or
                        (wb.final_fuelCmd == t_cf2_fuel and
                            t_cf2_fuel.injectionPulseWidth < t_cf1_fuel.injectionPulseWidth)))) and
            ((not t_cf3_fault and not t_cf2_active) implies
                (wb.modeChannelBroadcaster.currentMode == SystemOperatingMode::Normal_Mode and
                    not wb.modeChannelBroadcaster.inhibitDrivingControl and
                    wb.final_airCmd == t_cf1_air and
                    wb.final_fuelCmd == t_cf1_fuel));

        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::SystemArbiter_WhiteBox {
            in cf1_airCmd = t_cf1_air;
            in cf1_fuelCmd = t_cf1_fuel;
            in cf2_airCmd = t_cf2_air;
            in cf2_fuelCmd = t_cf2_fuel;
            in cf3_airCmd = t_cf3_air;
            in cf3_fuelCmd = t_cf3_fuel;
            in cf2_active = t_cf2_active;
            in cf3_faultActive = t_cf3_fault;
        }

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_Arbiter {
        subject arbiter_RefinementTestHarness : Arbiter_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_cf1_air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in v_cf1_fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in v_cf2_air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in v_cf2_fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in v_cf3_air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in v_cf3_fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in v_cf2_active : Boolean;
        in v_cf3_fault : Boolean;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-18';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-07';
        }

        action harness : Arbiter_RefinementTestHarness {
            in t_cf1_air = v_cf1_air;
            in t_cf1_fuel = v_cf1_fuel;
            in t_cf2_air = v_cf2_air;
            in t_cf2_fuel = v_cf2_fuel;
            in t_cf3_air = v_cf3_air;
            in t_cf3_fuel = v_cf3_fuel;
            in t_cf2_active = v_cf2_active;
            in t_cf3_fault = v_cf3_fault;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. CF2 (긴급 토크 개입 및 보상 기능) 정제·등가성 검증
    // =========================================================================

    // 2.1 병렬 토크 저감 제어 (UF2_1)
    action def UF2_1_RefinementTestHarness {
        in test_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::ParallelTorqueReduction_WhiteBox {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
        }

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            (test_req.requestedTorqueDelta < 0.0 [N * m] and
                test_req.messageAge <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::commDelayThreshold) implies
            (wb.airCmd.massFlow < wb.airCmd.previousMassFlow and
                wb.fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth < wb.fuelCmd.previousPulseWidth and
                wb.receiveInterventionReq.executionTime +
                    wb.calcReductionTorque.executionTime +
                    wb.limitActuatorOutput.executionTime <=
                    TimingConceptsAndLimits::interventionStageBudget);

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF2_1 {
        subject uF2_1_RefinementTestHarness : UF2_1_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-03';
        }

        action harness : UF2_1_RefinementTestHarness {
            in test_req = v_req;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // 2.2 통신 지연 보상 제어 (UF2_2)
    action def UF2_2_RefinementTestHarness {
        in test_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::CommunicationDelayCompensation_WhiteBox {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
        }

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            (test_req.messageAge > TimingConceptsAndLimits::commDelayThreshold and
                test_req.messageAge <= TimingConceptsAndLimits::commTimeout) implies
            (wb.airIntake.massFlow == wb.airIntake.previousMassFlow and
                wb.fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth == wb.fuelCmd.previousPulseWidth);

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF2_2 {
        subject uF2_2_RefinementTestHarness : UF2_2_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-15';
        }

        action harness : UF2_2_RefinementTestHarness {
            in test_req = v_req;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // 2.3 고착 대응 연료 차단 제어 (UF2_3)
    action def UF2_3_RefinementTestHarness {
        in test_stuckConfirmed : Boolean;
        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::ActuatorStuckFuelCut_WhiteBox {
            in stuckConfirmed = test_stuckConfirmed;
        }
        out isRefined : Boolean =
            test_stuckConfirmed implies
            (SafeStateLogic::IsFuelCutOff(wb.fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth) and
                wb.detectActuatorStuck.executionTime + wb.executeEmergencyFuelCut.executionTime <=
                TimingConceptsAndLimits::stuckCutStageBudget);
        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF2_3 {
        subject uF2_3_RefinementTestHarness : UF2_3_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_stuckConfirmed : Boolean;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-14';
        }

        action harness : UF2_3_RefinementTestHarness { in test_stuckConfirmed = v_stuckConfirmed; }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. CF3 (하드웨어 안전 모니터링 및 페일세이프) 정제·등가성 검증
    // =========================================================================

    // 2.4 긴급 개입 해제 및 제어 복귀 (UF2_4)
    action def UF2_4_RefinementTestHarness {
        in test_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::InterventionReleaseControl_WhiteBox {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
        }

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            test_req.requestedTorqueDelta >= 0.0 [N * m] implies
            (wb.detectReleaseReq.isReleased and wb.executeRelease.releaseFlag);

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF2_4 {
        subject uF2_4_RefinementTestHarness : UF2_4_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-19';
        }

        action harness : UF2_4_RefinementTestHarness {
            in test_req = v_req;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // 2.5 개입 요청 무결성 가드 (UF2_5)
    action def UF2_5_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        in test_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;
        in test_integrityError : Boolean;

        out isEquivalent : Boolean = bb.validatedRequest == wb.validatedRequest;

        action bb : CF2::CF2_5 {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
            in integrityError = test_integrityError;
        }
        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::InterventionRequestIntegrityGuard_WhiteBox {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
            in integrityError = test_integrityError;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF2_5 {
        subject uF2_5_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF2_5_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;
        in v_integrityError : Boolean;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-05';
        }

        action harness : UF2_5_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_req = v_req;
            in test_integrityError = v_integrityError;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. CF3 (하드웨어 안전 모니터링 및 페일세이프) 정제·등가성 검증
    // =========================================================================

    // 3.1 하드웨어 마찰 및 지연 보정 제어 (UF3_1)
    action def UF3_1_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        private import SafeStateLogic::*;

        in test_baseAir : IntakeAirFlow;
        in test_baseFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in test_sensor : EngineSensorData;
        in test_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        out isEquivalent : Boolean =
            wb.airIntake.massFlow >= test_baseAir.massFlow - maxAirCorrection and
            wb.airIntake.massFlow <= test_baseAir.massFlow + maxAirCorrection and
            wb.fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth >=
            test_baseFuel.injectionPulseWidth - maxFuelCorrection and
            wb.fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth <= test_baseFuel.injectionPulseWidth + maxFuelCorrection;

        action bb : CF3::CF3_1 {
            in baseAirCmd = test_baseAir;
            in baseFuelCmd = test_baseFuel;
            in sensor = test_sensor;
            in combustionStatus = test_combustion;
        }
        action wb : FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::HardwareDelayAndFrictionCompensation_WhiteBox {
            in baseAirCmd = test_baseAir;
            in baseFuelCmd = test_baseFuel;
            in sensor = test_sensor;
            in combustionStatus = test_combustion;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF3_1 {
        subject uF3_1_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF3_1_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_baseAir : IntakeAirFlow;
        in v_baseFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in v_sensor : EngineSensorData;
        in v_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-22';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-08';
        }

        action harness : UF3_1_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_baseAir = v_baseAir;
            in test_baseFuel = v_baseFuel;
            in test_sensor = v_sensor;
            in test_combustion = v_combustion;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // 3.2 치명적 센서 결함 페일세이프 제어 (UF3_2)
    action def UF3_2_RefinementTestHarness {
        in test_faultCause : FaultCause;

        action wb : FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::CriticalSensorFaultFailSafe_WhiteBox {
            in faultCause = test_faultCause;
        }

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            test_faultCause != FaultCause::NoFault implies
            (SafeStateLogic::IsFullCutOff(
                    wb.airIntake.massFlow,
                    wb.fuelCmd.injectionPulseWidth
                ) and
                wb.dashboard.status == DashboardWarningStatusEnum::Critical and
                wb.classifyFault.executionTime + wb.shutdownSystem.executionTime <=
