                    TimingConceptsAndLimits::failSafeStageBudget);

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF3_2 {
        subject uF3_2_RefinementTestHarness : UF3_2_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_faultCause : FaultCause;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-07';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-25';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-20';
        }

        action harness : UF3_2_RefinementTestHarness { in test_faultCause = v_faultCause; }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // 3.3 이중화 센서 편차 감시 (UF3_3)
    action def UF3_3_RefinementTestHarness {
        private import SafeStateLogic::*;

        in test_pedalPosition : AccelerationPedalPosition;
        in test_throttleFeedback : ThrottlePositionFeedback;

        action wb : FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::RedundantSensorDeviationMonitoring_WhiteBox {
            in pedalPosition = test_pedalPosition;
            in throttleFeedback = test_throttleFeedback;
        }

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            wb.pedalFaultFlag ==
                IsChannelDeviated(
                    test_pedalPosition.pvs1Percentage,
                    test_pedalPosition.pvs2Percentage,
                    redundancyDeviationLimit
                ) and
            wb.throttleFaultFlag ==
                IsChannelDeviated(
                    test_throttleFeedback.tps1Percentage,
                    test_throttleFeedback.tps2Percentage,
                    redundancyDeviationLimit
                );

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF3_3 {
        subject uF3_3_RefinementTestHarness : UF3_3_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_pedalPosition : AccelerationPedalPosition;
        in v_throttleFeedback : ThrottlePositionFeedback;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-02';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-06';
        }

        action harness : UF3_3_RefinementTestHarness {
            in test_pedalPosition = v_pedalPosition;
            in test_throttleFeedback = v_throttleFeedback;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // 3.4 스로틀 추종 편차 감시 (UF3_4)
    action def UF3_4_RefinementTestHarness {
        private import ThrottleStuckLogic::*;

        in test_throttleCmd : ThrottlePositionCommand;
        in test_throttleFeedback : ThrottlePositionFeedback;

        action wb : FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::ThrottleTrackingMonitoring_WhiteBox {
            in throttleCmd = test_throttleCmd;
            in throttleFeedback = test_throttleFeedback;
        }

        out isRefined : Boolean =
            wb.trackingFaultFlag ==
            IsThrottleDeviated(
                test_throttleCmd.openingPercentage,
                test_throttleFeedback.openingPercentage,
                stuckDeviationLimit
            );

        assert constraint { isRefined }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF3_4 {
        subject uF3_4_RefinementTestHarness : UF3_4_RefinementTestHarness;

        in v_throttleCmd : ThrottlePositionCommand;
        in v_throttleFeedback : ThrottlePositionFeedback;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-04';
        }

        action harness : UF3_4_RefinementTestHarness {
            in test_throttleCmd = v_throttleCmd;
            in test_throttleFeedback = v_throttleFeedback;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isRefined);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 4. CF4 (파워트레인 캘리브레이션) 및 CF5 (진단·유지보수) 검증
    // =========================================================================

    // 4.1 메모리 접근 인가 (UF4_1)
    action def UF4_1_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        in test_calibrationReq : CalibrationRequest;
        in test_expectedCredential : Integer;
        in test_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in test_vehicleSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;

        out isEquivalent : Boolean =
            bb.isAuthorized == wb.isAuthorized and bb.authStatus == wb.authStatus;

        action bb : CF4::CF4_1 {
            in calibration_Req = test_calibrationReq;
            in expectedCredential = test_expectedCredential;
            in ignition = test_ignition;
            in vehicleSpeed = test_vehicleSpeed;
        }
        action wb : FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel::MemoryAccessAuthorization_WhiteBox {
            in calibration_Req = test_calibrationReq;
            in expectedCredential = test_expectedCredential;
            in ignition = test_ignition;
            in vehicleSpeed = test_vehicleSpeed;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF4_1 {
        subject uF4_1_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF4_1_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_calibrationReq : CalibrationRequest;
        in v_expectedCredential : Integer;
        in v_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in v_vehicleSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-16';
        }

        action harness : UF4_1_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_calibrationReq = v_calibrationReq;
            in test_expectedCredential = v_expectedCredential;
            in test_ignition = v_ignition;
            in test_vehicleSpeed = v_vehicleSpeed;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // 4.2 맵 데이터 무결성 검증 (UF4_2)
    action def UF4_2_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        in test_calibrationReq : CalibrationRequest;
        in test_isAuthorized : Boolean;

        out isEquivalent : Boolean =
            bb.isIntegrityOk == wb.isIntegrityOk and bb.verifyStatus == wb.verifyStatus;

        action bb : CF4::CF4_2 {
            in calibration_Req = test_calibrationReq;
            in isAuthorized = test_isAuthorized;
        }
        action wb : FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel::DataIntegrityVerification_WhiteBox {
            in calibration_Req = test_calibrationReq;
            in isAuthorized = test_isAuthorized;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF4_2 {
        subject uF4_2_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF4_2_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_calibrationReq : CalibrationRequest;
        in v_isAuthorized : Boolean;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-11';
        }

        action harness : UF4_2_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_calibrationReq = v_calibrationReq;
            in test_isAuthorized = v_isAuthorized;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // 4.3 비유효 접근 거절 및 롤백 (UF4_3)
    action def UF4_3_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        in test_calibrationReq : CalibrationRequest;
        in test_isAuthorized : Boolean;
        in test_integrityFailed : Boolean;

        out isEquivalent : Boolean = bb.errorStatus == wb.errorStatus;

        action bb : CF4::CF4_3 {
            in calibration_Req = test_calibrationReq;
            in isAuthorized = test_isAuthorized;
            in integrityFailed = test_integrityFailed;
        }
        action wb
            : FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel::InvalidAccessRejectionAndRollback_WhiteBox {
            in calibration_Req = test_calibrationReq;
            in isAuthorized = test_isAuthorized;
            in integrityFailed = test_integrityFailed;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF4_3 {
        subject uF4_3_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF4_3_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_calibrationReq : CalibrationRequest;
        in v_isAuthorized : Boolean;
        in v_integrityFailed : Boolean;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-16';
        }

        action harness : UF4_3_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_calibrationReq = v_calibrationReq;
            in test_isAuthorized = v_isAuthorized;
            in test_integrityFailed = v_integrityFailed;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // 5.2 센서 영점 조정 루틴 (UF5_2)
    action def UF5_2_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        in test_diagReq : DiagnosticRequest;
        in test_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in test_vehicleSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;

        out isEquivalent : Boolean = bb.zeroStatus == wb.zeroStatus;

        action bb : CF5::CF5_2 {
            in diag_Req = test_diagReq;
            in ignition = test_ignition;
            in vehicleSpeed = test_vehicleSpeed;
        }
        action wb : FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel::ZeroPointCalibrationRoutine_WhiteBox {
            in diag_Req = test_diagReq;
            in ignition = test_ignition;
            in vehicleSpeed = test_vehicleSpeed;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF5_2 {
        subject uF5_2_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF5_2_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_diagReq : DiagnosticRequest;
        in v_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in v_vehicleSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-17';
        }

        action harness : UF5_2_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_diagReq = v_diagReq;
            in test_ignition = v_ignition;
            in test_vehicleSpeed = v_vehicleSpeed;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // 5.4 고장 메모리 저장 (UF5_4)
    action def UF5_4_EquivalenceTestHarness {
        in test_faultCause : FaultCause;

        out isEquivalent : Boolean =
            bb.dtcRecord.dtcCode == wb.dtcRecord.dtcCode and
            bb.dtcRecord.storageTime == wb.dtcRecord.storageTime;

        action bb : CF5::CF5_4 {
            in confirmedFaultCause = test_faultCause;
        }
        action wb : FW_CalibrationDiagnosticsWhiteBoxModel::FaultMemoryStorage_WhiteBox {
            in confirmedFaultCause = test_faultCause;
        }

        assert constraint { isEquivalent }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF5_4 {
        subject uF5_4_EquivalenceTestHarness : UF5_4_EquivalenceTestHarness;

        in v_faultCause : FaultCause;

        objective equivalenceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-09';
        }

        action harness : UF5_4_EquivalenceTestHarness {
            in test_faultCause = v_faultCause;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isEquivalent);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 6. FE 수준 상태 및 아키텍처 검증
    // =========================================================================

    verification def Verify_IgnitionOffReset {
        doc
        /* 크루즈 활성, 긴급 개입, 치명 고장 상태에서 점화 OFF를 인가한 HILS
         * 시나리오의 관측값을 검사합니다. DTC 메모리는 리셋 전후 값을 대조합니다.
         */
        subject egas : EgasSystem;

        in v_storedDtcCodeBefore : Integer;

        objective resetObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-24';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-19';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-21';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-23';
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(
            egas.ecu.ccState == CruiseControlStateEnum::Off and
            egas.ecu.confirmedFaultCause == FaultCause::NoFault and
            egas.ecu.storedDtcCode == v_storedDtcCodeBefore
        );
    }

    verification def Verify_CruiseStateTransitions_Off {
        doc
        /* 점화 OFF 또는 Off 요청 시나리오 절차를 실행한 뒤 Off 상태를 확인합니다. */
        subject egas : EgasSystem;

        objective cruiseOffObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-23';
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(
            egas.ecu.ccState == CruiseControlStateEnum::Off
        );
    }

    verification def Verify_CruiseStateTransitions_Active {
        doc
        /* 제동·가속 페달이 없는 상태에서 유효한 Set 또는 Resume 절차를 실행한 뒤 Active를 확인합니다. */
        subject egas : EgasSystem;

        objective cruiseActiveObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-23';
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(
            egas.ecu.ccState == CruiseControlStateEnum::Active
        );
    }

    verification def Verify_CruiseStateTransitions_Suspended {
        doc
        /* Active에서 Cancel, 제동 또는 가속 페달 입력 절차를 실행한 뒤 Suspended를 확인합니다. */
        subject egas : EgasSystem;

        objective cruiseSuspendedObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-23';
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(
            egas.ecu.ccState == CruiseControlStateEnum::Suspended
        );
    }

    verification def Verify_ExtensibilityInspection {
        doc
        /* 신규 외부 제어기 인터페이스 추가 후 중재 핵심 로직 변경 라인 수와 경로 예산 검사 결과를 입력받아 검사합니다. */
        subject egas : EgasSystem;

        in arbiterCoreChangedLineCount : Integer;
        in pathBudgetsMet : Boolean;

        objective extensibilityObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-12';
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(
            arbiterCoreChangedLineCount == 0 and pathBudgetsMet
        );
    }
} // package FV_FunctionalEquivalenceVerificationModel

##02. Functional Viewpoint/4-2. FV Performance Constraints Verification Model .sysml##
package FV_PerformanceConstraintsVerificationModel {
    doc
    /* [성능 제약조건 정합성 검증 모델 (Performance Constraints Conformance Model)]
     * 본 패키지는 블랙박스 수준에서 정의된 전체 성능/지연시간 제약조건(Latency Budget)이 
     * 화이트박스 파이프라인의 하위 소기능 실행 시간(Execution Time) 합산에 의해 
     * 정합성 있게 충족(Conformance)되는지를 검증합니다.
     */

    private import FB_FunctionalBlackBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::*;
    private import RV_InterfaceModel::*;
    private import VerificationCases::*;
    private import ReqCommon::*;
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import TimingConceptsAndLimits::*;

    @MetaInformation {
        version = "v0.1";
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. UF1_1 수동 가속 제어 성능 정합성 검증 (총 지연시간 50ms)
    // =========================================================================

    action def UF1_1_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in test_accel : AccelerationIntent;

        // 블랙박스 및 화이트박스 실행
        action bb : CF1::CF1_1 {
            in ignition = test_ignition;
            in accel = test_accel;
        }
        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::ManualAccelerationControl_WhiteBox {
            in ignition = test_ignition;
            in accel = test_accel;
        }

        // 성능 정합성 여부 산출: 하위 기능 실행 시간 합이 전체 허용 지연시간(latency) 이하인지 확인
        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            wb.filterPedalInput.executionTime +
            wb.calcManualTorque.executionTime +
            wb.genEngineCommand.executionTime <=
            cf1PathBudget; // bb.accel.latency → 상수

        // 성능 제약조건 단정 (초과 시 위반)
        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF1_1_Performance {
        // 1) 검증 대상 시스템(Subject) 지정
        subject uF1_1_PerformanceTestHarness : UF1_1_PerformanceTestHarness;

        // 2) 검증 입력 파라미터 선언
        in v_ignition : IgnitionState;
        in v_accel : AccelerationIntent;

        // 3) 검증 목적(Objective) 지정
        objective performanceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-13';
            verify requirement 'SYSR-08';
        }

        // 4) 테스트 하네스 실행 및 입력값 주입
        action harness : UF1_1_PerformanceTestHarness {
            in test_ignition = v_ignition;
            in test_accel = v_accel;
        }

        // 5) PassIf를 사용하여 VerdictKind 타입 결과 반환
        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isPerformanceMet);
    }

    action def UF1_2_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_targetSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;
        in test_dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback;
        in test_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::CruiseControlDriving_WhiteBox {
            in targetSpeed = test_targetSpeed;
            in dynamics = test_dynamics;
            in combustionSystemStatus = test_combustion;
        }

        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            test_targetSpeed > 0.0 [km / h] implies
            wb.genEngineCommandOut.executionTime <=
                TimingConceptsAndLimits::combustionComputeBudget;

        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF1_2_Performance {
        subject uF1_2_PerformanceTestHarness : UF1_2_PerformanceTestHarness;

        in v_targetSpeed : ISQ::SpeedValue;
        in v_dynamics : VehicleDynamicsFeedback;
        in v_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        objective performanceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-08';
        }

        action harness : UF1_2_PerformanceTestHarness {
            in test_targetSpeed = v_targetSpeed;
            in test_dynamics = v_dynamics;
            in test_combustion = v_combustion;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isPerformanceMet);
    }

    action def UF1_3_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_overrideTriggered : Boolean;
        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::BrakeOverrideIntervention_WhiteBox {
            in overrideTriggered = test_overrideTriggered;
        }
        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            test_overrideTriggered implies
            wb.detectBrake.executionTime + wb.arbitrateOverride.executionTime <=
            overrideStageBudget;
        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. UF2_1 병렬 토크 저감 제어 성능 정합성 검증 (총 지연시간 15ms)
    // =========================================================================

    action def UF2_1_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        action bb : CF2::CF2_1 {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
        }
        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::ParallelTorqueReduction_WhiteBox {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
        }

        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            wb.receiveInterventionReq.executionTime +
            wb.calcReductionTorque.executionTime +
            wb.limitActuatorOutput.executionTime <=
            interventionResponseBudget;

        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF2_1_Performance {
        subject uF2_1_PerformanceTestHarness : UF2_1_PerformanceTestHarness;

        in v_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        objective performanceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-03';
        }

        action harness : UF2_1_PerformanceTestHarness {
            in test_req = v_req;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isPerformanceMet);
    }

    action def UF2_4_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::InterventionReleaseControl_WhiteBox {
            in torqueIntervention = test_req;
        }

        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            test_req.requestedTorqueDelta >= 0.0 [N * m] implies
            wb.detectReleaseReq.executionTime + wb.executeRelease.executionTime <=
                TimingConceptsAndLimits::interventionResponseBudget;

        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF2_4_Performance {
        subject uF2_4_PerformanceTestHarness : UF2_4_PerformanceTestHarness;

        in v_req : TorqueInterventionRequest;

        objective performanceObjective {
            verify requirement 'SYSR-19';
        }

        action harness : UF2_4_PerformanceTestHarness {
            in test_req = v_req;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isPerformanceMet);
    }

    action def UF2_3_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_stuckConfirmed : Boolean;
        action wb : FW_UF2_WhiteBoxModel::ActuatorStuckFuelCut_WhiteBox {
            in stuckConfirmed = test_stuckConfirmed;
        }
        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            test_stuckConfirmed implies
            wb.detectActuatorStuck.executionTime + wb.executeEmergencyFuelCut.executionTime <=
            stuckCutStageBudget;
        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. UF3_1 하드웨어 마찰 및 지연 보정 제어 성능 정합성 검증 (총 지연시간 10ms)
    // =========================================================================

    action def UF3_1_PerformanceTestHarness {
        private import SafeStateLogic::*;

        in test_baseAir : IntakeAirFlow;
        in test_baseFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in test_sensor : EngineSensorData;
        in test_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        action bb : CF3::CF3_1 {
            in baseAirCmd = test_baseAir;
            in baseFuelCmd = test_baseFuel;
            in sensor = test_sensor;
            in combustionStatus = test_combustion;
        }
        action wb : FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::HardwareDelayAndFrictionCompensation_WhiteBox {
            in baseAirCmd = test_baseAir;
            in baseFuelCmd = test_baseFuel;
            in sensor = test_sensor;
            in combustionStatus = test_combustion;
        }

        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            wb.monitorSensorError.executionTime +
            wb.calcCorrectionValue.executionTime +
            wb.applyCorrection.executionTime <=
            compensationBudget;

        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    verification def Verify_UF3_1_Performance {
        subject uF3_1_PerformanceTestHarness : UF3_1_PerformanceTestHarness;

        in v_sensor : EngineSensorData;
        in v_combustion : CombustionSystemStatus;

        objective performanceObjective {
            requirement {
                doc
                /* UF3_1: 정상 상태에서 마찰 보정 및 액추에이터 제어 인가까지의 지연시간(10ms) 충족 검증 */
            }
        }

        action harness : UF3_1_PerformanceTestHarness {
            in v_baseAir : IntakeAirFlow;
            in v_baseFuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
            in test_sensor = v_sensor;
            in test_combustion = v_combustion;
        }

        return verdict : VerdictKind = PassIf(harness.isPerformanceMet);
    }

    action def UF3_2_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_faultCause : FaultCause;
        action wb : FW_UF3_WhiteBoxModel::CriticalSensorFaultFailSafe_WhiteBox {
            in faultCause = test_faultCause;
        }
        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            test_faultCause != FaultCause::NoFault implies
            wb.classifyFault.executionTime + wb.shutdownSystem.executionTime <= failSafeStageBudget;
        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    action def Arbiter_PerformanceTestHarness {
        in test_inCF1_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in test_inCF1_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in test_inCF2_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in test_inCF2_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in test_inCF3_Air : IntakeAirFlow;
        in test_inCF3_Fuel : FuelIgnitionCommand;
        in test_inCF2_Active : Boolean;
        in test_inCF3_Fault : Boolean;
        action wb : FW_UF1_WhiteBoxModel::SystemArbiter_WhiteBox {
            in cf1_airCmd = test_inCF1_Air;
            in cf1_fuelCmd = test_inCF1_Fuel;
            in cf2_airCmd = test_inCF2_Air;
            in cf2_fuelCmd = test_inCF2_Fuel;
            in cf3_airCmd = test_inCF3_Air;
            in cf3_fuelCmd = test_inCF3_Fuel;
            in cf2_active = test_inCF2_Active;
            in cf3_fault = test_inCF3_Fault;
        }
        out isPerformanceMet : Boolean =
            test_inCF3_Fault implies
            wb.collectInputs.executionTime +
            wb.evaluatePriority.executionTime +
            wb.broadcastOutput.executionTime <=
            arbiterBudget;
        assert constraint { isPerformanceMet }
    }

    action def PedalPath_BudgetCheckHarness {
        doc
        /* 페달 경로: 주기 샘플링 + UF1_1 + UF3_1(후처리) + 중재기 + 여유 <= pedalResponseBudget (SHR-08, SYSR-13) */
        out isMet : Boolean = IsPathBudgetMet(
            pedalResponseBudget,
            pedalSamplingDelay,
            cf1PathBudget + compensationBudget,
            arbiterBudget,
            minTimingMargin
        );
        assert constraint { isMet }
    }

    action def OverridePath_BudgetCheckHarness {
        doc
        /* 오버라이드 경로: UF1_3 + 중재기 + 여유 <= overrideResponseBudget (SYSR-01b) */
        out isMet : Boolean = IsPathBudgetMet(
            overrideResponseBudget,
            overrideSamplingDelay,
            overrideStageBudget,
            arbiterBudget,
            minTimingMargin
        );
        assert constraint { isMet }
    }

    action def InterventionPath_BudgetCheckHarness {
        doc
        /* 개입 경로: UF2_4 + UF2_1 + 중재기 + 여유 <= interventionResponseBudget (SHR-04-1/2, SYSR-03) */
        out isMet : Boolean = IsPathBudgetMet(
            interventionResponseBudget,
            interventionSamplingDelay,
            integrityGuardBudget + interventionStageBudget,
            arbiterBudget,
            minTimingMargin
        );
        assert constraint { isMet }
    }

    action def StuckCutPath_BudgetCheckHarness {
        doc
        /* 고착 차단 경로: UF2_3 + 중재기 + 여유 <= stuckCutResponseBudget (SYSR-14) */
        out isMet : Boolean = IsPathBudgetMet(
            stuckCutResponseBudget,
            stuckCutSamplingDelay,
            stuckCutStageBudget,
            arbiterBudget,
            minTimingMargin
        );
        assert constraint { isMet }
    }

    action def FailSafePath_BudgetCheckHarness {
        doc
        /* 페일세이프 경로: UF3_2 + 중재기 + 여유 <= failSafeResponseBudget (UC3 vc5) */
        out isMet : Boolean = IsPathBudgetMet(
            failSafeResponseBudget,
            failSafeSamplingDelay,
            failSafeStageBudget,
            arbiterBudget,
            minTimingMargin
        );
        assert constraint { isMet }
    }

    action def CompensationPath_BudgetCheckHarness {
        doc
        /* 보정 경로(연소 피드백 트리거): UF3_1 + 중재기 + 여유 <= compensationResponseBudget (SHR-02-2) */
        out isMet : Boolean = IsPathBudgetMet(
            compensationResponseBudget,
            compensationSamplingDelay,
            compensationBudget,
            arbiterBudget,
            minTimingMargin
        );
        assert constraint { isMet }
    }

    action def FaultToleranceTimeBudgetCheckHarness {
        private import ThrottleStuckLogic::*;
        private import SafeStateLogic::*;
        doc
        /* 확정 시간 + 차단 응답 <= FTTI (S4, S7의 확정 시간과 안전 목표 FTTI의 정합) */
        out isStuckFttiMet : Boolean = stuckConfirmTime + stuckCutResponseBudget <= stuckFtti;
        out isSensorFttiMet : Boolean =
            sensorFaultConfirmTime + failSafeResponseBudget <= sensorFaultFtti;
        out isOverheatFttiMet : Boolean =
            overheatConfirmTime + failSafeResponseBudget <= overheatFtti;
        assert constraint { isStuckFttiMet and isSensorFttiMet and isOverheatFttiMet }
    }
} // package FV_PerformanceConstraintsVerificationModel

//          * 그대로 유지된 채, 하위 SW 개발자가 즉시 코드로 변환할 수 있는
//          * 완벽한 '실행 가능한 명세(Executable Specification)'가 완성됩니다.

##EgasViews.sysml##
package RV_ViewsAndViewpoints {
    private import SysideViews::*;
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import StandardViewDefinitions::*;

    // 이전에 정의된 모델 패키지들을 가져옵니다.
    private import RV_ContextModel::*;
    private import RV_StakeHolderModel::*;
    private import RV_StakeholderRequirementModel::*;
    private import RV_StakeholderReqValidationModel::*;
    private import RV_UseCases::*;
    private import ViewAndViewpintCommon::*;
    private import RV_StakeHolderConcernModel::*;

    // ==========================================
    // 1. Viewpoint 정의 (관심사 및 목적 프레이밍)
    // ==========================================

    viewpoint def StakeholderPerspective {
        doc
        /* [이해관계자 뷰포인트]
         * 시스템의 핵심 이해관계자와 그들의 우선순위, 역할(카테고리)을 한눈에 파악하기 위한 목적을 가집니다.
         */
    }

    viewpoint def RequirementTraceabilityPerspective {
        doc
        /* [요구사항 추적성 뷰포인트]
         * 이해관계자 요구사항(SHR)이 시스템의 유스케이스(UC)와 검증 케이스(Verification)에 
         * 어떻게 할당(Allocate/Satisfy)되고 검증(Verify)되는지 누락 없이 추적하기 위한 목적입니다.
         */
    }

    // 01. Objectives Table

    view egas_objective_table : TVD::TableView {
        viewpoint stakeholderPerspective : StakeholderPerspective;
        satisfy stakeholderPerspective;

        // 대상 요소 지정: RV_StakeHolderModel 패키지 내부의 모든 요소
        expose RV_Objectives::*;
        filter @ SysML::RequirementDefinition;

        view Name :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::name; }
        view Doc :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::documentation; }
        view Heritage :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::heritage; }
        view Owner :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::owner; }
        attribute :>> includeLibraryElements = true;

        :>> TableViewDefinitions::TableView::columnOrder = (Name, Doc, Heritage, Owner);
    }

    // 02-1 시스템 컨텍스트
    view egas_system_context : InterconnectionView {
        expose RV_ContextModel::EgasContext::*;
        filter not (@ NotShownInView);

        attribute fileName = "02_1_egas_system_context";
        attribute depth = 2;
        attribute fileType = "png";
        // render asTreeDiagram;
    }

    view egas_system : GeneralView {
        expose RV_ContextModel::*;

        attribute fileName = "02_1_egas_system";
        attribute depth = 2;
        attribute fileType = "png";
        // render asTreeDiagram;
    }

    view egas_system_main_action : ActionFlowView {
        expose RV_ContextModel::EgasSystem::EgasMainContinuousOperation::*;
        // filter not (@ NotShownInView);

        attribute fileName = "02_1_egas_system_main_action";
        attribute depth = 2;
        attribute fileType = "png";
        // render asTreeDiagram;
    }

    view egas_system_state : StateTransitionView {
        expose RV_ContextModel::EgasSystem::egasBehavior::*::**;
        // filter not (@ NotShownInView);

        attribute fileName = "02_1_egas_system_state";
        attribute depth = 2;
        attribute fileType = "png";
        // render asTreeDiagram;
    }

    // 02-2 Actors
    view egas_system_actors : GeneralView {
        expose RV_ActorModel::*;
        filter not (@ NotShownInView);

        attribute fileName = "02_1_egas_system_actors";
        attribute depth = 2;
        attribute fileType = "png";
        render Views::asTreeDiagram;
    }

    view egas_system_interfaces : GeneralView {
        expose RV_InterfaceModel::*;
        filter not (@ NotShownInView);

        attribute fileName = "02_1_egas_system_interfaces";
        attribute depth = 2;
        attribute fileType = "png";
        render Views::asTreeDiagram;
    }

    // 03-1 Stakeholder Concerns
    view egas_stakeholder_concern_table : TVD::TableView {
        viewpoint stakeholderPerspective : StakeholderPerspective;
        satisfy stakeholderPerspective;

        // 대상 요소 지정: RV_StakeHolderModel 패키지 내부의 모든 요소
        expose RV_StakeHolderConcernModel::*;
        filter @ SysML::ConcernDefinition;

        view Name :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::name; }
        view Doc :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::documentation; }
        view Heritage :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::heritage; }
        view Owner :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::owner; }

        attribute :>> includeLibraryElements = true;

        :>> TableViewDefinitions::TableView::columnOrder = (Name, Doc, Owner, Heritage);
    }

    view egas_stakeholder_requirement_table : TVD::TableView {
        viewpoint stakeholderPerspective : StakeholderPerspective;
        satisfy stakeholderPerspective;

        // 대상 요소 지정: RV_StakeHolderModel 패키지 내부의 모든 요소
        expose RV_StakeholderRequirementModel::stakeholderRequirementSet::*;
        filter hastype SysML::RequirementDefinition or hastype SysML::RequirementUsage;
        attribute :>> includeAbstractElements = true;
        // attribute :>> exposeFeaturesAndHeritage = true;
        attribute :>> maxRowHeight = 300;

        view ID :> columnViews {
            attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::declaredShortName;
            attribute :>> defaultValue = "id";
            attribute :>> columnWidth = 100;
        }
        view Name :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::name; }
        view Doc :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::documentation; }
        view Heritage :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::heritage; }
        view Owner :> columnViews { attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::owner; }
        view Description :> columnViews {
            attribute :>> featureToRender = CT::attributeValue {
                attribute attributeName = "description";
            }
            attribute :>> defaultValue = "n/a";
            attribute :>> columnWidth = 304;
        }

        :>> TableViewDefinitions::TableView::columnOrder = (
            ID, Name, Doc, Owner, Heritage, Description
        );
    }
} // package RV_ViewsAndViewpoints

##SysideViews.sysml##
// Copyright (c) 2026 Sensmetry, UAB. All Rights Reserved.
// Version 0.10.1
library package <SV> SysideViews {
    enum def <CT> ColumnType {
        enum declaredShortName {
            doc
            /* The short name of the element as declared in the model.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an explicit element, but **does not** currently support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum declaredName {
            doc
            /* The name of the element as declared in the model.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an explicit element, but **does not** currently support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum reqId {
            doc
            /* Alias for the declaredShortName.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an explicit element, but **does not** currently support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum shortName {
            doc
            /* The short name of the element after the semantic resolution.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an implicit element -> **does not** support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum name {
            doc
            /* The name of the element after the semantic resolution.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an implicit element -> **does not** support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum qualifiedName {
            doc
            /* The qualified name of the element.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an implicit element -> **does not** support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum owner {
            doc
            /* The owner of the element.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an implicit element -> **does not** support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum attributeValue {
            doc
            /* The value of the attribute that is a feature of the requirement.
             *
             * **Fields**
             *
             * `attributeName` is a required field that must be set to the name of the attribute.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an explicit element -> **supports** editing through the UI.
             *
             * While SysML v2 supports having unnamed attributes, for the table view a non-empty `attributeName`
             * is required as attribute is a feature that can be inherited.
             */
            attribute attributeName default null;
        }
        enum namedFeatureValue {
            doc
            /* The value of the feature that is a feature of the requirement.
             *
             * **Fields**
             *
             * `featureName` is a required field that must be set to the name of the feature.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * While this is an explicit element, it **does not** currently support editing
             * through the UI for clarity reasons (because '=' is overloaded for both assignment
             * and binding in SysML, depending on the feature type).
             *
             * Similar to `attributeValue`, the table view requires non-empty `featureName` due to inheritence
             */
            attribute featureName default null;
        }
        enum documentation {
            doc
            /* The documentation of the element. It takes ALL the documentations of this element
             * and concatenates them together.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an implicit element -> **does not** support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum specificDocumentation {
            doc
            /* The named documentation of the element. It takes the documentation with the name
             * specified in the `documentationName` attribute. For nameless docs, set the `documentationName` to "".
             *
             * **Fields**
             *
             * `documentationName` is a required field that must be set to the name of the documentation.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an explicit element -> **supports** editing through the UI.
             */
            attribute documentationName default "";
        }
        enum heritage {
            doc
            /* The heritage of the element. It recursively checks through each element from the heritage
             * and concatenates them together. E.g. the heritage of `b`:
             * ```
             * part def A;
             * part def B :> A;
             * part b : B;
             * ```
             * will be "B, A".
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an implicit element -> **does not** support editing through the UI.
             */
        }
        enum <CL> constraintLanguage {
            enum def <ConT> ConstraintType {
                enum plain;
                enum assumed;
                enum asserted;
                enum required;
            }
            doc
            /* The language representation of a specific named constraint, e.g.:
             *
             * require constraint named_constraint {
             * language "English" <...>
             * }
             *
             * **Fields**
             *
             * `constraintName` is a required field that must be set to the non-empty name of the constraint.
             *
             * `constraintType` is a required field that must be set to the type of the constraint. Currently supported types are:
             * - `plain`: The constraint is regular constraint.
             * - `assumed`: The constraint is an assumed constraint.
             * - `asserted`: The constraint is an asserted constraint.
             * - `required`: The constraint is a required constraint.
             *
             * `constraintLanguage` is a required field that must be set to the language of the constraint.
             *
             * **Editing**
             *
             * This is an explicit element -> **supports** editing through the UI.
             *
             * Similar to `attributeValue`, the table view requires non-empty `constraintName` due to inheritence
             */
            attribute constraintName default null;
            attribute constraintType default ConstraintType::plain;
            attribute constraintLanguage default "English";
        }
    }

    enum def <Dir> MatrixTraceabilityDirection {
        enum undirected {
            doc
            /* Row can either be in source or target, and column is the other of the two.
             * Shows "✔" if relationship fits.
             */
        }
        enum row2col {
            doc
            /* Source is row, target is column. Shows "↗" if relationship fits. */
        }
        enum col2row {
            doc
            /* Source is column, target is row. Shows "↙" if relationship fits. */
        }
    }

    enum def <Rep> MatrixElementRepresentation {
        enum name;
        enum shortName;
        enum qualifiedName;
    }

    view def ContentView {
        attribute includeLibraryElements default false;
        attribute includeReferenceElements default false;
        attribute includeAbstractElements default false;
        attribute exposeFeaturesAndHeritage default false;
    }

    package <TVD> TableViewDefinitions {
        view def <TV> TableView :> ContentView {
            private import ScalarValues::*;
            attribute maxRowHeight : Integer default 125;
            attribute globalFilter : String default null;
            ref columnOrder :> columnViews [0..*];
            view columnViews [0..*] {
                doc
                /* Subset these views to set what columns you'd like to see in your table.
                 *
                 * The order is preserved, unless overwritten in `columnOrder`
                 */
                attribute featureToRender : CT default CT::qualifiedName {
                    doc
                    /* With this attribute you can choose what aspect of the element to visualise in the column.
                     *
                     * The choices are available in the `ColumnType` enum.
                     */
                }
                attribute defaultValue default "" {
                    doc
                    /* Set what default value to show if no feature value or no documentation is found.
                     *
                     * Default is empty cell.
                     *
                     * Default Value is rendered as grayed out and in italics.
                     */
                }
                attribute columnWidth : Integer default 150;
                attribute columnFilter : String default null;
            }
        }

        view def <HTV> HierarchicalTableView :> TableView {
            filter as SysML::Definition istype SysML::Definition;
            attribute :>> exposeFeaturesAndHeritage = true;
            attribute defaultCollapse default true;
        }
    } // package <TVD> TableViewDefinitions

    package <MVD> MatrixViewDefinitions {
        view def <MV> MatrixView {
            view rowView : ContentView [1] {
                doc
                /* Inside this view use `expose` and `filter` to specify what you want to see
                 * as rows in the matrix.
                 */
                attribute representation : Rep default Rep::name;
            }
            view columnView : ContentView [1] {
                doc
                /* Inside this view use `expose` and `filter` to specify what you want to see
                 * as columns in the matrix.
                 */
                attribute representation : Rep default Rep::name;
                attribute verticalColumnNames default false;
            }
            view cellView : ContentView [1] {
                doc
                /* Inside this view use `expose`, `filter`, and `direction` to specify what you want to see
                 * as cells in the matrix.
                 */
                attribute :>> includeReferenceElements default true;
                attribute direction : Dir default Dir::undirected;
                attribute showWeakTraceability default false;
                attribute portsAreTransparent default false;
                /* If true, connections to ports will be treated as direct connections to the port's owner */
                filter (
                    as SysML::ConnectionUsage hastype SysML::ConnectionUsage or
                    as SysML::AllocationUsage hastype SysML::AllocationUsage or
                    @ RequirementDerivation::derivation
                );
            }
        }

        abstract view def EditableMatrixView :> MatrixView {
            view :>> rowView {
                filter as SysML::Definition istype SysML::Definition;
            }
            view :>> columnView {
                filter as SysML::Definition istype SysML::Definition;
            }
            view :>> cellView {
                attribute :>> exposeFeaturesAndHeritage = true;
            }
        }

        view def ConnectionMatrixView :> EditableMatrixView {
            view :>> cellView {
                filter (
                    as SysML::ConnectionUsage hastype SysML::ConnectionUsage and
                    not (@ RequirementDerivation::derivation)
                );
            }
        }

        view def AllocationMatrixView :> EditableMatrixView {
            view :>> cellView {
                filter as SysML::AllocationUsage hastype SysML::AllocationUsage;
            }
        }

        view def RequirementMatrixView :> EditableMatrixView {
            view :>> cellView {
                filter @ RequirementDerivation::derivation;
            }
        }
    } // package <MVD> MatrixViewDefinitions
} // library package <SV> SysideViews

##view_Common.sysml##
package ViewAndViewpintCommon {
    metadata def NotShownInView;
} // package ViewAndViewpintCommon
